# Changelog

All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.

## [2.8.39] - 2026-10-06 — camp-rename knife: everything the player sees now says 「营地」 (not 「本营」), and "the camp has **no HP ceiling** — it may exceed its initial 6" is promoted from a remark to a **rule**, with the dev-only clamp that had been hiding it removed (`src/` runtime touched ⇒ version number + tag; **anchors unchanged**)

- Four rulings arrived in one message the same day; three were buildable and are in this knife — the rename (24 files · 73 lines, incl. the three trigger labels), the **one-way legacy aliases** on the Excel read side (an old workbook cell saying 「本营…」 still imports, and the written form is always 「营地…」), and the no-ceiling rule (both test-arena roads stopped clamping at `INITIAL_BASE_HP`; the resolution engine never had a camp ceiling in the first place, so **no match rule changed**). Full account = **PROJECT_HANDOFF §12-110** (11 sub-points), readings = **§9「2.8.39」**, vocabulary ledger = **ARCH_MAP §H11** + §H10 reproduction record.
- Standing criterion worth keeping: **renaming a word the player can both read and type back into the engine is a data-format change, not a copy change** — the same round must add read-side aliases and a whole-tree scan nail (new `src/data/campTerminology.test.ts`: 「本营」 may live only in the alias file and its test, 3 keys + 1 comment = exactly 4 occurrences; one more or one less turns the suite red).
- The first 整备 correction round wrote a false fact into the ledger ("a hit clears it"); this round read the assignment sites line by line, hung an in-place correction pointer on §12-108⑥ and shipped a permanent witness (`src/core/armingClearRoutes.test.ts`, 3 cases) — there are exactly three clear roads: turn start, dying to that blow, arms depot emptied. The **engine-side MOVE gap** (the UI blocks moving an armed general, the resolver does not) is left as-is and registered as **awaiting the user's ruling**, not quietly patched.
- The other two rulings deliberately did **not** get code: the 将领池 numbers the user gave are mutually impossible (25+15=40≠30, 15+10=25≠15, and either pool breaks his own ≤⅓ quota), so the plain-language restatement gate is held until he says which three numbers count; the range-changing skill line is **filed on paper with zero implementation** because the rule shape ("+1 range" = reach one more zone, or unlock a restricted zone?) was never given.
- Gates **measured on the final tree, twice over**: the mid-registration `v2.9`→`v2.8.39` label fix changed `PLAYER_GLOSSARY.md`, the glossary is a one-way projection with a byte-equality guard, so the workbook was re-projected and **the whole gate set re-run** — check 0 errors / eslint 0 errors · 29 legacy warnings / `test:coverage` exit 0 **1,117 tests · 94 files** ×2 passes, coverage `Stmts 63.79–64.20 · Branch 55.40–55.89 · Funcs 54.69–55.04 · Lines 68.88–69.33（定稿树 3 趟，登记末趟＝登记文档改完之后、提交之前那棵树）`, floors 42/34/34/47 clear, untightened / build **2,087,567 B · gzip 613.53 kB**. **C graded, run as A**: both anchors two rounds each, byte-identical after stripping the two timing lines ⇒ **B17 {"1":95,"2":205} · B18 {"1":93,"2":207} unchanged, no rename**; the pre-reprojection captures compare byte-equal too. Real-browser E2E passed (rules page + board DOM: 0 old-word hits, camp heart shows `❤️6` with no denominator, `❤️20`/`❤️11` after live-store edits while `baseMaxHp` stays 6); **no screenshot** — the tab was hidden, so the witness is DOM text.
- **CI = PENDING and that is the honest state**: feat `85e259d` + this docs commit + tag `v2.8.39` are local only — the push waits for the user (version-line, 将领池 numbers, range shape, push-or-not).

## [Unreleased] - 2026-10-06 — glossary-answer intake round: the user filled 13 cells of the 「你的理解」 column by hand in `词汇表.xlsx`, this round harvested them into the source of truth and fixed the channel that would have destroyed them (tooling + docs: **`src/` untouched ⇒ no version number, no tag**)

- **The hazard was real, not theoretical**: the workbook is a one-way projection of `PLAYER_GLOSSARY.md` (`npm run glossary-xlsx` overwrites it wholesale), and the guard test pins **byte equality** — so the moment the user typed answers into Excel the suite turned red on its own (`AssertionError: expected 43541 to be 36392`, 1 failed / 3 passed), and one more command would have silently deleted everything they wrote. Both workbooks were backed up outside the repo first (md5 recorded), and a cell-by-cell diff proves the user touched **only** the third column (13 cells), never a content column.
- What changed: the 13 answers are now **verbatim** in `PLAYER_GLOSSARY.md`'s third column (rows 17/18/19/20/22/23/28/35/36/37/43/64/103), the generator's `clean()` maps `<br>` → a real line break (a multi-line answer had nowhere to live before — the user's 将领池 reply is three lines), the guard test gained a probe witness (`甲<br>乙<br>丙` ⇒ one cell, three lines, table not split) plus `<br>` added to the markdown-residue list. Regenerated workbook read back = **13/13 survived byte-for-byte**.
- Standing criterion worth keeping: **any sheet handed to the user for filling is a projection, so a path to harvest their entries back into the source of truth must exist before they fill it**; and抄录 of a user's own words must be proven by a byte comparison, not by eye (my first pass normalised their full-width quotes “ ” to straight ones and the read-back caught it).
- The 13 answers were **classified, not implemented** — matches-the-code / wording-vs-doc / genuinely conflicts-with-code / overturns a previous ruling / brand-new gameplay / is-a-question — each with a `file:line` witness in **PROJECT_HANDOFF §12-108⑥**; nothing in the game was altered for them, and each consequential one goes through the plain-language restatement gate first. Two of them overturn the user's own v2.8.18 本营/营地 split and one (将领池 30/15, 群雄 ≤1/3, 晋 exemption) does not exist today.
- Gates **measured this round, not inherited** (a script and a test changed): check 0 errors / eslint 0 errors · 29 legacy warnings / build OK **2,087,394 B — byte-identical size to v2.8.38**, the structural witness that the playable build did not change / `test:coverage` exit 0 **twice**, **1,103 tests · 91 files** (+1 = the new probe). Coverage `Stmts 63.48–63.50 · Branch 55.25–55.28 · Funcs 54.39–54.43 · Lines 68.53–68.53（同树 2 趟，登记第 2 趟）`, floors 42/34/34/47 clear. **Anchors not re-run and stated as such** (B-grade: `src/` and both pool input surfaces are untouched) — B17/B18 keep their v2.8.38 readings.
- Full account = **PROJECT_HANDOFF §12-108** + **§9「词汇表答复接入轮」** (the single landing point for CI) + the §3 index line; dual histories one chapter each.
- **CI was NOT green, and that is registered, not smoothed over**: #249 (run `37421161809`, sha `68c5e14`) = `test (22)` success / `test (24)` success / **`lint` job failure at the step 「Security audit (high or above blocks)」** (`npm audit --audit-level=high`) / `build` **skipped** (blocked by lint — never written up as "ran"). Causality excluded on evidence: the two commits touch neither `package.json` nor `package-lock.json` (empty diff), and the previous run #248 was green ⇒ the red came from a **freshly published upstream advisory**, `source-map-js 1.0.0–1.2.1`, **GHSA-68fv-2mgg-jv7q** (event-loop DoS), reproduced locally as exit 1 / 1 high vulnerability.
- **Fix = `729d060`** — `overrides: { "source-map-js": "1.2.2" }` in `package.json` (same technique as the earlier uuid pin), because it's a transitive dep of four packages (`@tailwindcss/node`, `postcss`, `magicast`, `css-tree`); `npm audit --audit-level=high` now exits 0 with 0 vulnerabilities. No blanket `npm audit fix` — it would have dragged unrelated versions into a small incident. Gates re-run on the final tree: check 0 / eslint 0 errors · 29 legacy warnings / build OK at **2,087,394 B — same size again, proving the package isn't in the bundle ⇒ the playable build is unchanged ⇒ this fix also takes no version number and no tag** / `test:coverage` exit 0 twice, **1,103 tests · 91 files**, coverage `Stmts 63.96–63.97 · Branch 55.85–55.88 · Funcs 54.82–54.87 · Lines 69.03–69.03（定稿树 2 趟，登记第 2 趟）`.
- Honest observation, no invented cause: coverage shifted ~0.5 pt across the dependency change (pre-fix 63.48–63.50 → final tree 63.96–63.97). Test/file counts, the exit-0 gate and the floors are unaffected; the mechanism was **not** explained and is registered as an observation — another on-site confirmation of "coverage is not an anchor criterion" (§12-58④). Full account = **§12-109**.
- **Closeout** (same-day backfill, one-round cap): `729d060` (fix) + `7ff84d8` (registration docs) went up in **one direct push** — no proxy borrowed, `http.proxy` re-checked empty — so only the head sha has a run: **CI = run `37422969293` (#250, sha `7ff84d8`) — see PROJECT_HANDOFF §9「词汇表答复接入轮」**, the single landing point for the step-level reading (four jobs all success, 0 failed steps, and the step that broke #249 — 「Security audit (high or above blocks)」 — is now green; `build` genuinely ran this time, unlike #249 where it was `skipped`). `e03e11b`/`729d060` have no independent run; `68c5e14`'s only run is the red #249. The backfill commit's own run is not chased.

## [Unreleased] - 2026-10-05 — two standing rules from the user (documentation round: no `src/`, no tests, no anchors, **no version number and no tag**)

- **Rule 1 — official generals' skill content belongs to the user**: verbatim ruling 「你自己生成的技能内容，可以随便更改，但是不能更改我的官方将领技能」. Scope = the three content fields of the 95 cards in `src/data/generals.ts` (skill name / card text / effect payload); what I author myself (editor `D-*`/`G-*`, repo fixture `D-fix-*`) stays freely changeable. This is stricter than v2.8.6's three-layer editor lock — that one constrains the player's editor, this one constrains **me**. Construction consequence written as a hard gate: a diff touching an official card's content field must cite a user quote in HANDOFF §12, otherwise the knife doesn't open.
- **Compliance audit before accepting the rule** (so the rule doesn't silently imply past work was wrong): the six recent commits touching `generals.ts` (`6b07902` `b80bc32` `6787314` `07d2240` `b1024b7` `a82bcfe`) were read at `-U0` — type unions, editor dropdown labels, comments, and new optional fields only; the seventh, docs `d748083`, is a printed-line change that had its own user quote ⇒ **no official content field was rewritten without a quote ⇒ nothing to roll back**. Consequence for the long-standing pending question: 关平【龙吟】 card text is closed as **leave it alone**; contrast 董卓【崩坏】 (v2.8.33 `d748083`), which was changed **because the user gave a quote for it** — the rule is about provenance, not about whether card text may ever differ from the engine.
- **Rule 2 — coverage is registered as a range plus which measurement run produced it**: verbatim 「覆盖率数字以后登记一段范围＋第几趟量」. Format = four metrics on one line + interval + run number, e.g. `Stmts 63.9–64.2 · Branch 55.6–56.0 · Funcs 54.7–55.0 · Lines 68.9–69.3（同树 7 趟，登记第 7 趟）`; a single measurement is written honestly as 「单趟、无区间」 rather than a fabricated interval. **Three things unchanged**: floors 42/34/34/47, the `test:coverage` exit-0 hard gate, and "coverage is not an anchor criterion" (§12-58④ — anchors read only the two pools' game results). The interval only states plainly that the numbers jitter; it relaxes nothing.
- Registration surfaces: HANDOFF **§12-106** (both quotes verbatim, boundary, audit, honest note) + **§9** new entry for this round + **§3** one index line; `AGENTS.md` two standing rules; **both** copies of the process doc (repo `PROJECT_RELEASE_PIPELINE.md` Step 1 + 特殊口径, and the local skill `three-kingdoms-tripartite-registration`) changed in the same round; dual histories one chapter each.
- Honest note kept in the record: I searched the local session logs for an earlier occurrence of this wording and **found none** — the ruling reached me as a mobile screenshot, so "I said this before" is not something I can evidence locally, and I don't pretend otherwise.
- **Same-day correction to Rule 1 (user re-drew the line)**: 「关平【龙吟】的卡面文字也是你生成的，可以改」, and when offered two ways to place the boundary the user picked **「按谁写的划」** — by authorship, not by which file a field happens to live in. My first registration protected "the three content fields of the official cards", which silently assumed those texts were the user's; measured otherwise: the 39 description lines dedupe to **37 unique strings** whose first-introducing commits are all content-era cuts (`1262a4d`×19 · `f961b26`×9 · `b154463`×3 · `487e024`×2 · `156cb21`×2 · `d1acb3e`×1 · `d748083`×1), while the 2.0.0 commit `540bd7b` contains **zero** `const SK_` blocks. So the protected set is what the user wrote — the `createGeneral` roster fields (name / faction / max HP / skill names / title) and any skill whose meaning comes from the user's own table or words — and the printed descriptions plus their effect payloads, being mine, are editable **on condition that every change is registered** (which line, why). Wording may be sharpened; changing what a skill *does* is still a gameplay knife under the three-gate protocol. Provenance recipe worth keeping: search the **exact description literal**, never the skill name — 「龙吟」 was already in the 2.0.0 roster, and searching names returns the wrong owner (this round's own pitfall, §12-107⑤). Consequence: 关平【龙吟】 re-classified from "not mine ⇒ leave it" to **"may change — the user chose 暂不改，保持现状"**, so no file was edited for it; nothing historical needs rolling back, and §12-106's published text keeps its wording with a bracketed pointer instead of a rewrite. Full account = **PROJECT_HANDOFF §12-107** + **§9「官方内容归属改判轮」** + the §3 index line. **Closeout** (same-day backfill, one-round cap): registration commit docs `9777367` (7 doc files, `src/`/tests/data/glossary untouched ⇒ gates and both anchors inherited from v2.8.38); pushed **direct** (no proxy borrowed, `http.proxy` re-checked empty); **CI = run `37266016249` (#247, sha `9777367`) — see PROJECT_HANDOFF §9「官方内容归属改判轮」**, the single landing point for the step-level reading; the backfill commit's own run is not chased.
- **Closeout**: registration commit docs `2b86d34` (6 doc files; `git status` re-confirmed `src/`/tests/data/glossary untouched ⇒ gates and both anchors inherited from v2.8.38, not re-measured and not claimed as new). Push: direct connect timed out (`Failed to connect to github.com:443 after 21103 ms`), netstat showed 10808 listening ⇒ `gh-rescue` took the **sys** route and went through first try (`6bf94c8..2b86d34`); `http.proxy` re-checked empty in both `--local` and `--global`. **CI = run `37263009102` (#243, sha `2b86d34`) — see PROJECT_HANDOFF §9「两条规则轮」, the single landing point for the step-level reading.** One-round cap: the backfill commit's own run is not chased.

## [2.8.38] - 2026-10-05 — chain if-then knife: the compiler now accepts a third effect-combination mode (「链式若-则」), and the round also books an anchor drift that three earlier knives left unregistered

- What the knife does: `skillCompiler.ts` gains an `effectMode === 'chain'` branch — several effects sharing one trigger signature compile into ONE definition carrying `effectChain: true`, and `SkillTriggerBridge` translates them **in order, stopping at the first item that fails to produce** (the "if… then…" meaning: no 则 ⇒ nothing further runs). New named skip reason `PASSIVE_CHAIN_UNSUPPORTED` for the `passive` × chain combination; `dataTypes.ts` gains the optional `effectChain` flag; the editor dropdown label is the only player-facing text touched.
- **Grade = B, structural no-op**: zero cards in the official 95 and in the DIY fixture carry `effectMode:'chain'` ⇒ the new branch cannot be reached by existing content ("wired ≠ configurable", no serial number counted — §12-104⑩ d). Gates on the final tree: check 0 / **1,102 tests · 91 files** (+5, all in the new `chain` describe) / eslint 0 errors · 29 legacy warnings unchanged / build **2,087,394 B** (`2.8.38` ×1, `2.8.37` ×0). Glossary deliberately untouched ⇒ 168 entries, xlsx not re-projected, the md↔xlsx guard test green is the witness that nothing was silently reworded. No browser E2E, stated: no reachable UI surface and no new HUD wording — unit nails are not passed off as real clicks.
- **The round's real finding (the reason this entry is longer than the knife)**: the pre-work baseline run did NOT match the registered anchors (official 105/195 → **95/205**, fixture 107/193 → **93/207**). A structural no-op cannot cause that, so each tree was re-measured by checkout-bisect: **v2.8.34 reproduces the old pair verbatim, the drift starts at v2.8.35's camp-middle-slot knife `5e5ce12`** (an A-grade gameplay change — seat 1 can no longer deploy a general) whose docs commit `c6c0abc` touched only the version header. Per "value moved ⇒ rename": **B16 → B17 {"1":95,"2":205}**, **B15 → B18 {"1":93,"2":207}** (first-hand single-round measurements on the HEAD tree, won=300 / VIOLATIONS=0, per-faction sub-accounts in §H10); old readings turn historical at v2.8.34 and are never deleted. **From now on a non-content cut's hard anchor = B17 AND B18 both verbatim.**
- Honest boundaries registered, not smoothed: B17/B18 are **one round each** (this is a bookkeeping correction round, and the numbers cross-check against the per-tree bisect runs — that justification does not license skipping the two-round A-grade run in future); v2.8.36/37's index lines claimed "B13/B15 verbatim", which was both a wrong anchor name and already drifted ⇒ corrected **in place with brackets**, original text not backdated; no gate readings were invented for the versions being caught up.
- Criteria added = **PROJECT_HANDOFF §12-105** (an A-grade gameplay knife's closeout must update BOTH the §3 index line and the §H10 status column in the same round; a baseline mismatch is a witness, not noise, and the first action is checkout-bisect attribution — never adjusting the registered numbers; catch-up lines carry only provable facts). Anchor ledger = **PROJECT_ARCH_MAP §H10**; gates/anchors/CI = **§9「2.8.38」条** (CI **#240** run `37205263705`, sha `6b07902`, four jobs success / 0 failed steps, verified step-level via anonymous REST).
- **Closeout (written on the docs commit itself)**: registration commit `ed82afe` (8 doc files, `src/` untouched ⇒ gates/anchors inherited) pushed after a direct-connect timeout — first attempt failed all three routes, then succeeded **direct** via `gh-rescue` after re-measuring `api.github.com`＝200 and a 20 s backoff; `http.proxy` re-checked empty in both `--local` and `--global`. Its own run = **CI #241** `37224791343` — four jobs success / 0 failed steps, the single non-success being `test (24)`'s coverage upload (`skipped` = matrix de-dup, by design); per the one-round cap the backfill commit's run is not chased further. **Deviation registered, not fixed**: annotated tag `v2.8.38` (tag object `d9e5aa5`) points at the **feat** commit `6b07902`, whereas v2.8.30–v2.8.32 put the tag on the docs registration commit — the tag is already public, so it is left alone and the difference is written into §9/§3 (a published ref is not rewritten for tidiness).

## [2.8.32] - 2026-10-04 — 限定技额度刀: 「一局一次」becomes an engine **ledger** — all three activation roads read one predicate and write one accounting shape (A-grade criteria change; both anchors byte-identical, and that non-movement has a stated, checkable cause)

- All six user rulings landed, verbatim in **§12-104**①: quota is per **skill** (two limited skills on one general = two uses; one skill compiled into several definitions shares one quota), the exhausted entry stays listed but greyed with 「本局已用尽」, **no refund on negation** (structurally true — the ledger is append-only and has no rollback entry point, and no "negate a skill" mechanic exists at all), and **a failed activation costs nothing** (`producedCount === 0` ⇒ no write). 锁定技's other half is deliberately not done (nothing consumes it); 觉醒技 is archived word-for-word only — no awakening mechanic yet, one knife away.
- Single points: new leaf module `src/skills/skillQuota.ts` holds the **predicate** (`limitedQuotaAvailable` — scans the ledger for *any* entry with the same `limitKey`, **ignoring turn numbers**, so "one game" ≠ "one turn") and the **one producer of `SKILL_ACTIVATED` payloads** in the codebase. Key = `<general instance id>:<skill name>` (`skillCompiler.ts:226`); `passive` definitions intentionally carry no key (a always-on effect has no "one activation" moment). New rejection reason `SKILL_LIMIT_EXHAUSTED`; the turn-end road's "already used this turn" stays a **separate** rule.
- Two own-mistakes corrected: the response-ask road **does** write the same entry (a 2.3.1-era comment said it didn't), and grey-out reasons are now ordered by **irreversibility** (quota before turn) — caught by the independent recount (gate ③), not by me. Plus a real hole closed: a whole trigger chain reads state from **before** the write, so two hits on one limited skill would resolve twice and charge once ⇒ per-expansion `quotaSpent` set (`EngineDispatchFlow.ts:22-33`).
- UI: candidates and legal actions read the **same** predicate, so a greyed item can never be activatable. **Honest bound①**: "listed but greyed" only exists in the turn-end window in full mode — the reaction-ask window has no greyed shape (exhausted = never asked again); **bound②**: the reworded priority landed after the browser run, so the corrected text is pinned by unit tests only. Real hot-seat E2E passed on an editor-authored limited-skill general (deploy → activate → same-turn grey → next own turn 「本局已用尽」 → non-button `cursor-not-allowed`, zero side effects → smart mode opens no window at all).
- **A-grade, both anchors byte-identical after two rounds each**: B16 `{"1":105,"2":195}`, B15 `{"1":107,"2":193}`, and against v2.8.31's archive only two metadata lines differ (game bodies identical). Cause is a grep-able content fact: **zero** cards in the official 95 or the DIY fixture carry the 「限定技」 badge ⇒ quota-bearing definitions are never produced (8th "wired ≠ configurable") — the roads' reachability criteria did change, so the run wasn't downgraded. 1,097 tests／91 files (+27／+1: new `skillQuota.test.ts` 24 cases + 3 recompute-numbering nails and 4 assertion patches handed back from v2.8.31's recount), coverage floors untouched, build 2,082,599 B (+1,079), glossary 168 entries, xlsx re-projected 35,794→36,392 B, `package.json`/lock re-aligned to 2.8.32.

## [2.8.31] - 2026-10-04 — 受击搬家刀: one attack is now TWO beats — declare → 受击 layer → damage, so a "becoming the target" skill really answers **before** the damage lands (A-grade gameplay knife; the official anchor moved ⇒ renamed)

- Three user rulings close the thread the previous two rounds opened (**§12-103**①, verbatim): 受击 = becoming the target (fires before damage is computed, independent of that blow's damage or the owner's survival) / 受伤 = having taken damage (fires after, needs real HP loss); forced and manually-answered roads must follow the **same** rule, differing only in whether a human answers (方案二); and **甲** for the open question — *「A 砍 B，B 的"成为目标时"反伤先把 A 扎死了……甲：不算了」* ⇒ a pre-damage counter that kills the attacker **cancels the blow**.
- Implementation: new single derivation point `src/core/attackBlow.ts` (legality, reach, `getAttackValue`, both beats' event shapes; `AttackResolver` reduced to a delegator — **no second transition path**). The declare notice carries `attackStage:'declare'` + a container-derived `attackKey` (no rng consumption). **Deferral only when there are listeners** (`hasReactionListeners`, same derivation point as the duel opening, counting both roads) ⇒ with no listeners the stream stays byte-identical to 2.3.0 by construction.
- After the layer answers, the blow is **re-aimed and recomputed** (`resumeAttackBlow`) — damage numbers are pre-baked into `DAMAGE`, so reordering events could never change them. If the aim no longer holds it returns `[]`: zero events, no `ACTION_REJECTED`, no HP write. The deferred-resume loop in `TransitionCore` carries all three guards (cap 4, dedup keys, honest `CUSTOM{kind:'ATTACK_RESUME_LIMIT'}`) and only scans newly appended events per wave.
- **v2.8.30's settlement-side half-gate was wrong and is deleted module-and-all** (`core/ownerOnFieldGate.ts`, `pruneCancelledEffects`, replay pruning, the `ownerPresenceRequired`/`ownerGeneralId` payload keys); enforcement now lives at exactly **one** point (`SkillTriggerBridge.ownerCanActivate`, the 受伤 half). Withdrawal-by-deletion, not by adding a switch.
- **A-grade**: official anchor **B13 {"1":104,"2":196} MOVED ⇒ renamed B16 {"1":105,"2":195}** (only 魏/吴 rows shifted; B13 turns historical, readings kept), fixture anchor **B15 {"1":107,"2":193} byte-identical** — both causes probe-measured this round, for the first time both halves of §12-98① in one entry: official pool 69 ATTACK dispatches／4 deferred (⇒ genuinely changed a result) vs fixture pool 34／1 (⇒ **"reached and identical", not "not reached"**). 1070 tests／90 files, coverage floors untouched, single-file build 2,081,520 B (+1,063), glossary 168 entries, xlsx re-projected 35,882→35,794 B.
- Real-browser hot-seat E2E: 孙策【激昂】deployed at **1 HP** and attacked — the ask window opens before the damage and the draw lands before `DAMAGE` on a killing blow; **honest limit**: 甲案, the 🚫跳过 road and the 强制发动 road are pinned by unit tests only (natural clicks cannot produce them). Registered, not silently改字: 董卓【崩坏】's printed 「（伤害结算后落账，不减当次伤害）」 is now **false** — card-text rewording is a content decision awaiting the user.
- Full evidence = `PROJECT_HISTORY_AI.md` 本轮章 · rulings/criteria = **PROJECT_HANDOFF §12-103**（§12-102⑥ 自此结案＝甲）· contract = **PROJECT_ARCH_MAP §H9 第十六轮**＋§F `onBecomingTarget` 十二格表＋§H10 本轮锚记录 · gates/anchors/CI = §9「2.8.31」条（**CI = #229 run `37176948030`, sha `4255786`, 4 jobs success / 0 failed steps**; pushed via the one-off local proxy after direct connect timed out — config re-checked empty afterwards).

## [Unreleased — 受击／受伤定义封口轮] - 2026-10-04 — the two words are sealed, so half of v2.8.30 is reclassified as a wrong call (docs only: `src/` untouched ⇒ no version number, no tag)

The user owned the back-and-forth and closed it: *「按现在已经清晰的定义，受击＝成为目标，受伤＝受到伤害。被砍死的一刀里，受击可以发动，受伤不能发动。强制发动和手动发动都需要遵循这套规则，不能出现强制发动和手动发动不同的情况」* plus a standing rule wider than this case — *「无论是什么规则，强制发动和手动发动的处理都是必须一致的，区别只在于'需不需要玩家自行响应'」* (**§12-102**①④). Consequences: §12-101's "judge after the whole blow settles" now covers **only** the damage-taken class; §12-100's "the killing blow suppresses both classes" narrows to the damage-taken class; and v2.8.30's settlement-side gate — which cancels a becoming-target effect the owner had already fired before the damage — is **judged wrong and scheduled for removal**. Measured before writing any of that (throwaway probe test, deleted after): today the auto road resolves the counter **after** the source damage (`BEFORE_DAMAGE > TRIGGERED > DAMAGE(attack:2) > … > DAMAGE(skill:1)`), and the ask road only opens its cell once the whole blow has settled — so honouring "before the damage" is a **timing move on both roads**, not merely pulling a gate out, and the one thing that cannot be inferred (if the pre-damage counter kills the attacker, does the blow still land?) is **put to the user as an open question** before any knife is cut (§12-102⑤⑥). Glossary rows are deliberately left alone this round so docs and the shipped build don't contradict each other mid-flight; they change with the implementation knife, whose first gate is a plain-language restatement. v2.8.30's CI is backfilled here too: **#227 run `37138496998`** — four jobs success, 0 failed steps, one skipped step (matrix coverage upload, by design); readings land in §9 only.

## [2.8.30] - 2026-10-04 — 「离场不发动」执法刀: a general that is no longer on the field cannot activate a skill — **including** the 「强制发动」 (auto-activate) path

- The user's ruling (*「将领不在场上不能发动技能，被砍死了不算在场上，能发动就是空发，就算是「强制发动」不能发动。」* + judgement time = **after the whole blow settles** + an exemption for 「死亡时发动」/on-death skills) closes the one open question the previous round left on the books (§12-100⑨ 「待裁」 ⇒ **ruled: yes, cover it**).
- One rule, **two enforcement points**, because the auto path generates its effects against the *pre-settlement* state: emission side `SkillTriggerBridge.ownerCanActivate` (owner already gone when the event re-enters — the 「受到伤害后」 half) and settlement side **`core/ownerOnFieldGate.ts`** (effect generated while the owner lived, settled after its `DEATH` — the 「成为目标时反伤」 half). Both read the same fact; no second state-transition path was added.
- The exemption travels as one payload flag (`ownerPresenceRequired`, stamped once at emission, read once at settlement) so the "is this the owner's own death sound?" judgement has exactly one home. A cancelled effect neither applies **nor stays in the recorded event stream** (`pruneCancelledEffects`, object identity) — on screen and in the ledger it simply never happened, same shape as the ask path.
- **A-grade**: anchors **B13 {"1":104,"2":196}** and **B15 {"1":107,"2":193}** each two rounds, byte-identical after normalization *and* against the archived v2.8.29 captures ⇒ **no re-anchoring**, cause measured not assumed: the gate was consulted (68／123 flagged effect events; 19,479／19,462 total) and cancelled **0** — those pools contain no "owner dies in the same blow" shape yet, so this is "not reached", **not** "reached and identical" (§12-98①).
- Real-browser E2E in a hot-seat room, no dev password: lethal blow ⇒ counter-damage does not land while the on-death draw still does; non-lethal control ⇒ counter lands and the live payload shows `ownerPresenceRequired: true`. 1064 tests／90 files, coverage floors untouched, glossary 168 entries (xlsx re-projected 35,675→35,882 B, byte-stable), single-file build 2,080,457 B.
- Rulings verbatim + mechanism account = **PROJECT_HANDOFF §12-101**; contract line = **PROJECT_ARCH_MAP §H9 第十四轮**; anchor reproduction = **§H10**; full evidence = `PROJECT_HISTORY_AI.md` 本轮章 · gates/anchors/CI = §9 本轮条（**CI = #227, run `37138496998`** — pushed 2026-10-04 in the authorised five-commit batch, all four jobs success, 0 failed steps）.
- **Superseded the next morning (rules only, this build stays as shipped)**: the user sealed the two words — **受击 = becoming the target (fires *before* damage is computed), 受伤 = having taken damage (fires *after*)** — and ruled that in the blow that kills the owner **the becoming-target half may activate, the damage-taken half may not**, with the further standing principle that *forced and manually-answered activations must follow the same rule; the only difference is whether a human has to answer* (**§12-102**). That makes **this version's settlement-side gate (`core/ownerOnFieldGate.ts` + pruning the recorded event) the wrong half** — it cancels exactly the becoming-target class; it is scheduled for removal together with moving that timing in front of the damage on **both** roads. The emission-side gate (`ownerCanActivate`) covers the damage-taken half and stays.

## [Unreleased — 致命击规则登记轮] - 2026-10-03 (late night) — the last "known limitation" of the reaction-chain knife is reclassified as a **rule**, and one sentence I had inferred wrongly is removed from the player glossary (test+docs only: `src/` runtime untouched ⇒ no version number, no tag)

The user ruled, in answer to "what's next": *when a general is killed by a blow, its damage-taken **and** being-targeted skills cannot respond to that attack/skill — that was already the settled rule; and the knife order stays as agreed* (§12-100①). This wording is **wider** than the v2.8.22 entry it replaces, which only mentioned the damage-taken side, so it was measured rather than assumed: a throwaway probe tested both timings in both directions — not fatal ⇒ two cells open, source events literally `BEFORE_DAMAGE` / `INJURY`; fatal ⇒ zero cells **and** the effects those cells would have produced never happen (the 1-point recoil never lands, the drawn card never arrives). Mechanism, one read point: the ask is derived **after the whole dispatch settles** (`TransitionCore.ts:165`), and `reactionChain.ts::collectListeners` walks only living players' on-field generals; `BEFORE_DAMAGE` has no EventProcessor branch at all ⇒ its early position in the stream is **record order, not ask time** — which is also why the duel-closing "the dead one's tally is skipped" ruling (§H9 第七轮) is the same criterion, not a coincidence. Consequence registered honestly: the sentence I wrote into the glossary last round ("being-targeted is asked before the damage lands, so it still fires even if fatal") was **inferred from the timing's name and never measured — it was wrong**, and is now deleted; new standing criterion = *any "does this timing fire in this situation" claim needs a probe with both polarities before it reaches a document*. The probe became the permanent witness `src/skills/fatalBlowReaction.test.ts` (4 cases incl. two controls); the two `transitionEquivalence` comments were re-labelled "rule, not debt" with **no assertion touched**. Then the same criterion caught **a second wrong sentence of mine, this time inferred from code structure rather than from a timing's name**: I had written in ARCH_MAP that the `forced` (强制发动) branch "is decided by the same `collectListeners` structure". Measured first, it turned out **the opposite** — a `forced` definition never enters the ask queue, it resolves inside the settlement chain itself, so death cannot suppress it: with the same fatal fixture the counter-damage still lands (attacker 4→3) and the card is still drawn. So the ruling's reach is now stated honestly: **it covers the definitions that stop and ask the player, not the ones that fire without asking**, and whether the same gate should also apply to the auto path is **an open question for the user** (§12-100⑨⑩; boundary sentence added to the player glossary row, two more nails in the same witness file, paired with the two ask-path fatal cases as positive/negative controls). Gates: check 0 / **1061 · 90 files** / coverage 63.9·55.88·54.68·69, floors not raised / eslint 0 errors · 29 legacy warnings / build **2,079,638 B = same bytes as v2.8.29**. Anchors B-grade once each: **B13 {"1":104,"2":196}**, **B15 {"1":107,"2":193}**, won=300 · VIOLATIONS=0 — unchanged because *not reached*, not because unchanged (§12-98①). No browser E2E, deliberately: no UI wording or behaviour changed, and a unit pin is not passed off as a real click. Readings = **§9「致命击规则登记轮」条** (CI = PENDING, push awaits the user's word); judgement = **§12-100**; contract row = ARCH_MAP **§H9 第十三轮** + §H10 reproduction line; #71's two known limitations are now **both closed**. Next knife = badge knife 2, whose first gate is a plain-language restatement before any `src/`.

## [2.8.29] - 2026-10-03 (late night) — base-damage cap knife 「封」: skill damage hits the base for at most 1 point too, and that number now has exactly one home

The user's follow-up ruling (§12-96) was one sentence — *skill damage against a base is capped at 1 as well, and the number lives at the emission point only (the same slot normal attacks use)*. It now reads that way in code: new leaf module **`src/core/baseDamage.ts`** holds `BASE_MAX_DAMAGE_PER_HIT = 1` and `capDamageToBase` = `Math.min(1, amount)` (negatives and 0 pass through — the ruling set a ceiling, not a floor). Both roads that can reach a base take it from there: `AttackResolver` (already computed `Math.min(1, baseDamage)`, so **behaviour is byte-identical on that road**) and `SkillTriggerBridge`'s `DAMAGE` branch — a target id that resolves a seat but no general means the base, so it is clamped to 1, while hitting a general still pays the printed value. That clamp in the skill road is **the only behaviour change in this knife**. The settlement side books whatever the event carries and never recomputes; duels structurally cannot target a base (`findDuelParticipant` resolves generals only), so no branch was added for them. Evidence: 5 new tests in `src/core/baseDamageCap.test.ts` (**1055 · 89 files**; red-sensitivity proven — pulling the cap out turned exactly the 2 base nails red, nothing else), a real-browser hot-seat run where two click chains took a base 10→8→6 and the live-replay ledger shows `DAMAGE{attack,1,base_2}` → `TRIGGERED` → `DAMAGE{skill,1,base_2}` → `INJURY`(1), coverage floors not raised, eslint 0 errors · 29 legacy warnings, build 2,079,638 B (+74 over v2.8.28). **A-grade knife, and both anchors held word-for-word** (B13 {"1":104,"2":196}, B15 {"1":107,"2":193}, two rounds each, byte-identical after stripping timing lines) — registered honestly as *overturning* this knife's own pre-prediction ("大概率挪锚"): the cap was never triggered in those 600 games because no card in either pool deals >1 skill damage to a base, so "unchanged" means *not reached*, not *unmoving* (§12-98① — future content can move these anchors at any time). Glossary line 「本营」 updated and xlsx re-projected (168 terms unchanged). Readings = **§9「2.8.29」条**; judgement = **§12-98**; contract cells = PROJECT_ARCH_MAP §F `DAMAGE_TAKEN`/`BASE_HP` + §H10. **CI = PENDING** — eight commits and two tags (this one plus everything since `8209f45`, including tag `v2.8.28`) push in one go once github.com is reachable again.

## [2.8.27] - 2026-10-03 — damage-taken ledger knife (#26 = 2.8 knife 5): the correction sits BEFORE armour, and "being hurt" became one derived sound

One ordering lives in one file: `core/damageTaken.ts` = 原始伤害 → 受到伤害修正 → 护甲抵扣 → 最终扣血, and all three feed points
call that same pure function (normal attack pre-solves in `AttackResolver`, duel rounds pre-solve against a thread-local ledger in
`duelEvents.ts`, skill damage reads at settlement in `damageEvents.ts`); `core/armorDamage.ts` — the single armour exit — was not
touched by a byte, it now consumes the *result*. `DAMAGE_TAKEN` and the one-shot duration `thisDamage` entered the editor/Excel
vocabulary only once a read point existed (`WIRED_STAT_KEYS` 3→4), with a new named compile-time skip
`MODIFY_STAT_ONESHOT_KEY_UNSUPPORTED` for a one-shot mounted on a key that has no "moment it gets used". The user's
2026-10-03 re-ruling (只有掉血才算受到伤害) landed structurally rather than as a predicate: `INJURY` is derived from the real HP
delta after each hit settles, so armour eating the whole hit — or damage ≤ 0 — means the sound does not exist at all;
`DUEL_INJURY` was folded into that same `INJURY` and `onDamageTaken` now hears exactly one event (ask path and auto path can no
longer disagree by construction). 本营 is immune by shape: the "≤1 per hit" rule lives at the emission point
(`Math.min(1, baseDamage)`) and the base owns no ledger entry — while skill damage to the base stays uncapped, registered as 待裁.
Anchors: **B13 {"1":104,"2":196} byte-identical** (four normalised `cmp`, including a cross-version comparison against the archived
v2.8.26 capture) and the sample pool moved **108/192 → 107/193 ⇒ B14 retired, B15 established** — the two causes (semantic
re-ruling vs. a 13th fixture card) were separated by a dedicated 12-card probe. Tests 1008→1037 / 86→87 files. Full evidence =
PROJECT_HANDOFF §3 index line + §9 (single CI landing point) + §12-93, contract cells PROJECT_ARCH_MAP §F
`DAMAGE_TAKEN`/`DAMAGE_DEALT` + §H10, and the chapter in PROJECT_HISTORY_AI.md.

## [2.8.26] - 2026-10-02 — stat-modifier pipeline knife (#25 = 2.8 knife 4): skills can now change a number, and the card face is still never rewritten

The ledger is the feature. `EngineState.statModifiers` (optional array, class-A fact: it goes into replays, archives and
reconstruction; top-level `version` stays 1, `store/gameStateAdapter.ts` carries it field by field) plus ONE canonical event
`STAT_MODIFY{op:'ADD'|'REMOVE'}` — required by the `Event.ts:26-31` rule that every state-changing fact leaves its own trace,
with ids derived purely from the ledger (`sm:<seq>`, zero new RNG). Nothing is written back to a unit: a general's printed
`meleeAtk` stays 2 and the reading of 3 exists only in the instant of resolution, produced by the single attack read point
`getAttackValue(attacker, ranged, {ledger, target})` (shared by attack, duel and the panel) and by `effectiveMaxHp` (four
consumers, one view). `trigger.type:'passive'` enters the support table for the first time: it is NOT an activation, so it never
opens an ask window, never reads `forced`, never spends a per-turn slot; deploy derives the entry, death/leaving-field closes it
(including the half owed by somebody else's skill), re-entry is a brand-new sequence number. Max-HP shrink truncates current HP
without counting as damage or lost HP, and truncation to 0 dies on the spot under a new death cause `MAX_HP_ZERO` (no kill
record, no death-wisp, own draw compensation still paid, same corpse-handling path as damage death). Honest edges: only the
three keys with a real read point are selectable in the editor/Excel (`WIRED_STAT_KEYS`), so `DAMAGE_TAKEN`/`DAMAGE_DEALT` stay
registered-but-unwired for knife 5 — a key that "looks fireable but never fires" is the third lie this project forbids; four named
compile-time skips were added instead of silent intake. **No official card changes and no card gains a new effect**, and because
an empty ledger derives zero events, both anchors were predicted to be untouched and reproduced byte-identical: B13
`{"1":104,"2":196}` and B14 `{"1":108,"2":192}`, two rounds each, **and byte-equal to the captures taken before this knife
opened** ⇒ anchors not renamed. Tests 946→1008 (43 in three new files, 19 in existing ones), 83→86 files; five gates green
(check 0 / coverage 63.65·55.57·54.44·68.76 above unchanged floors / lint 0 errors, 29 inherited warnings / build 2,075,317
bytes = same as v2.8.25's final tree). Real-browser hotseat witness with zero developer passwords: the three editor cells →
deploy → exactly one `STAT_MODIFY` `sm:1`, panel reads 3 while the card prints 2, the ledger-less official general still hits for
2 in the same battlefield, the modified one hits for 3, and after death `statModifiers` is back to `undefined`. External review
(GPT, four questions, archived outside the repo) judged all four "adopt" and its two pre-registration criteria are now in the
contract: effective readings are a deterministic projection that must never enter a second persisted state, and knife 5 must hang
`DAMAGE_TAKEN` on a NEW canonical read stage *before* armour rather than rewriting `core/armorDamage.ts`'s single exit. Also new:
a cheap anchor-independent witness — a static entry-existence assertion that the official pool contains no `passive` trigger and
no `MODIFY_STAT` effect, which separates "the engine can" from "the content does". What is NOT closed, said out loud: the engine
still has no 【调离】 and no "return to hand" exit, so only death-type leaving is covered and the passive lifecycle loop is not
claimed closed (registered as the hard TODO in ARCH_MAP §F). Full evidence in `PROJECT_HISTORY_AI.md` this chapter; CI readings
only in HANDOFF §9 (PENDING — push awaits the user's explicit order); criteria §12-91; contract ARCH_MAP §F stat-modifier table.

## [2.8.21] - 2026-09-30

Listener-scope knife (#69, first of the three knives the user authorised with 「明白了就开工吧」): two
orthogonal axes now exist end to end — **「我听谁」** (listen only to myself / to my own seat / to the whole
board) and **「被谁指名」** (becoming the target of an *attack* vs of a *skill* vs either). They run through
the data model, the compiler, the trigger bridge's consumption path, the editor (two new dropdowns), Excel
(a 13th fixed column plus an 8th column per effect group) and the player glossary. **Both defaults are the
pre-expansion behaviour** (`self` / `attackTarget`), which is *why* the two anchors (B12 `{"1":106,"2":194}`,
B11 `{"1":108,"2":192}`) still reproduce — this is an A-grade knife (it touches the listener consumption path)
carrying a B-grade outcome.

- 「我听谁」 is a *second* axis, so it gets its own cell: `supportsListenerScope` deliberately does **not**
  live in `getTriggerSubOptions`, which hands out one sub-kind per timing. Orthogonal axes don't share a box.
- Widening a listener means re-checking, per trigger type, **whether the key is on the event at all**. The
  base-attack `BEFORE_DAMAGE` carries no `targetPlayerId`, so all three scopes stay silent there — that
  pre-existing fact is now pinned by a test rather than left to luck.
- Honest gap, stated in the UI itself: `skillTarget` / `anyTarget` are recordable but **have no emitter yet**
  (the skill-target notification path ships with #70/#71), so choosing them stores a true constraint that
  cannot ring in a game today. Capability before content, as with REVEAL/DECK_PLACE in 2.6.1.
- Renaming the *main* label (成为攻击目标时 → 成为目标时) is the dangerous kind of wording change: an old
  `.xlsx` would read as "didn't understand" and the skill would silently stop ringing, so the old wording is
  accepted as a **whole-cell alias** and never written back out.
- 872→900 tests (81 files). Details: HANDOFF §3 index row + §9 round entry, ARCH_MAP §F "监听扩面刀" +
  §H9 ④ + §H10 anchor ledger + §H11 vocabulary rows.

## [2.8.20] - 2026-09-30

Duel knife (#30, 2.8 knife 9): `DUEL` becomes the 10th settleable runtime effect primitive — a skill can
now make two generals trade blows, **three turns each, at most six calculations**, alternating, opening
side = the skill's owner. Each round uses the *striking* side's current attack value, armour still absorbs
1 point per 2, damage is typed **skill**, no card is spent, and the sequence truncates the moment someone
reaches 0 HP while the initiating skill's own remaining effects still run; death pay-out stays in the
normal FIFO queue. **There is still no "duel" button** — only a skill can start one, and no official or
fixture general has a `DUEL` effect, which is *why* both anchors (B12 `{"1":106,"2":194}`, B11
`{"1":108,"2":192}`) reproduce byte-identical under the strictest A-grade protocol.

- One event, one derivation point: the bridge emits a single `DUEL` carrying both participants; rounds are
  pre-solved inside `chainedConsequences` and re-applied by `EventProcessor` through a *constrained*
  head-of-queue right (continuous-settlement effects only). No new action type, no second transition path.
- Entry surface: new `VALUELESS_RUNTIME_TYPES = ['DUEL']` — a rule constant is not a fillable number, so the
  value row is absent entirely and the Excel cell is never read.
- **Open inference for the user's word**: whether "after taking damage"-class passives should ring *inside* a
  duel is not covered by the rulings; this build says no (§12-81 states the counter-ruling's blast radius).
- Details: HANDOFF §3 index row + §9 round entry (CI readings live there only), ARCH_MAP §F "DUEL 决斗流程原语"
  twelve-cell contract table + §H10 anchor ledger, full evidence in `PROJECT_HISTORY_AI.md`.

## [2.8.19] - 2026-09-30

Badge knife 1 (#43, first half of ruling 5): skill badges stop being a single mutually-exclusive
label and become what the user's 2026-09-30 correction says they are — several per skill, with the
meaning of each one stated the same way in the tooltip, the vocabulary sheet, the editor and the
import report. **Settlement is untouched by design**: enforcement of 锁定技 / 限定技 / 强制发动 is
knife 2, it needs the engine and it moves the anchors, so it waits for the user's word.

- **One source of truth, new file `src/domain/skillTags.ts`**: `TAG_SEPARATOR`, `skillTagMeanings`
  (five badges, each one sentence), `FORCED_MEANING`, `tagsOf()`, `formatTags()`,
  `parseSkillTagsCell()`, `triggerTypesOf()`, `tagTimingWarnings()`. Tooltips and
  `PLAYER_GLOSSARY.md` §四 read this one copy because four surfaces paraphrasing one concept is
  how the last three vocabulary drifts started.
- **The corrected semantics, verbatim from the ruling**: **锁定技 = cannot be nullled and cannot be
  changed** — it is *not* "fires automatically", that is the separate 「强制发动」 switch;
  **强制发动 = once its trigger and cost are met it fires / applies directly, no prompt**; and
  **neither is correlated with** "numeric change + permanent effect" — the three combine freely.
  A sample the user supplied is now a test: 闭月 carries 锁定技 **and** 遗计技 at once. A reverse pin
  fails the suite if 锁定技's own explanation ever grows the words "自动发动" again, because this
  class of defect does not crash — it teaches the next reader the wrong model.
- **Model: `tags?: SkillTag[]` is the only field anything writes; the legacy single `tag` stays
  readable forever.** Editor save deletes the inherited `tag` key so one skill never holds two
  badge ledgers. Why the read side may not be dropped: a lost badge **silently widens** behaviour
  (【限定技】 unread ⇒ "unlimited uses"), so unknown names are *named* on every import path instead of
  being discarded. This is §12-76①'s alias discipline, this time on a field.
- **Editor**: the badge row is now 「徽章（一枚技能可同时挂几枚）」 — five independent chips, lit ones
  show `✓`, each with its meaning on hover. The Excel 「技能标签」 dropdown became **non-blocking**
  (`showErrorMessage:false`) because this machine runs WPS and a blocking list makes
  「锁定技、遗计技」 impossible to type in the first place.
- **One splitter for all four import paths** (技能标签 cell / angle-bracket form in the skill name /
  auto-detect at the start of the description / free text). **A real fork found in our own repo
  and fixed**: the bracket branch accepted only the ideographic comma, so
  `反馈<锁定技 遗计技>` parsed as *no badges* and said nothing; it now delegates to
  `parseSkillTagsCell` (、,，;；/／+＋和与 and whitespace all split; `无`/empty ⇒ none).
- **Display**: codex card face and detail, board and test arena all render every badge through
  `formatTags(tagsOf(…))`; the badge filter matches "any of them" — clicking 遗计技 in the browser
  returned exactly one general (貂蝉).
- **Consistency check names, never edits (the restraint of this knife)**: `tagTimingWarnings`
  compares what the badge claims with the trigger timings actually recorded (登场技 ⇒ 登场时机,
 遗计技 ⇒ `onDeath`) and reports through two outlets — the editor card's `⚠ 徽章与时机对不上 — …`
  line and the import summary panel. Only **newly introduced** mismatches are reported (snapshot
  compare, so re-importing the same file never repeats the sentence), nothing is reported while no
  timing is recorded at all, and two broken badges produce two separate lines.
- **Tests +42 (819 → 861, 78 → 80 files)**: new `src/domain/skillTags.test.ts` (23), new
  `SkillEditor.badges.test.tsx` (10, including **export → re-import my own bytes ⇒ zero badges
  lost and zero changes reported**, with `file-saver` mocked so nothing ever downloads to the
  user's browser), `skillExcelParsers.test.ts` 33 → 42 (multi-badge cells, legacy `tag`-only
  records still count as "unchanged", three spellings of the bracket form, typo named not dropped).
- **Five gates (final tree)**: `check` 0 errors / `npm test` **861 · 80 files** / coverage

  29 legacy warnings with none added / `npm run build` single file **2,037,595 bytes · gzip

## [2.8.18] - 2026-09-30

The §八 wording knife from `PLAYER_GLOSSARY.md` — the seven unresolved "the screen says X, the rules compute Y" conflicts, plus the user's rulings 3 and 4 from the 2026-09-29 batch, quoted exactly: *"护甲：现在是 2 点护甲才挡 1 点伤害（单数不挡），是这样"* and *""装备"这个词：把技能描述改成"军备""*. **Pure presentation layer + authoring vocabulary: `src/core`, `src/action`, `src/rules` untouched, so both anchors stayed byte-identical** — that is this knife's external witness, not a self-claim.

- **军备 (ruling 4)**: three official card descriptions (强袭 / 枭姬 / 崩坏), the DIY fixture's 「样·缴械」, the trigger sub-label `cardSubLabels.equipmentLost` 「失去装备牌」⇒**「失去军备牌」**, the editor preview 「拆掉 N 张军备卡」, and **the AI operation log line** (`装备护甲 g1，2张` ⇒ `叠甲 g1，军备2张`, matching its neighbour `补给 g1，用卡2张`'s existing shape) all moved to the real card type. The card enum was always 粮草／材料／**军备** (`data/cards.ts:4`) — "装备" was never a card type and there is no equipment zone, so the Codex card needed no change.
- **只进不出, and why this alias is not optional**: `LEGACY_CARD_SUB_LABELS` + `normaliseLegacySubLabel` sit in front of the **strict** entry `readTriggerCell` only; the loose `strToTrigger` keeps its behaviour. The reason is recorded because it is the dangerous shape of a missing alias: if the sub-label fails to parse, `strToTrigger` returns `{type:'onCardLost'}` **without** `cardSubType`, which the compiler reads as "loses **any** card" — 枭姬 would silently widen from "draw two when a 军备 card is lost" to "draw two on any loss". Criterion: **before renaming a user-visible word, ask what the engine assumes when that cell no longer parses**; if the answer is "a bigger set", the alias must stay and be pinned by a test.
- **Three deliberate non-changes, with reasons on the record**: the editor **effect-type name** 「拆掉装备」 stays (ruling 4 scoped *skill descriptions*; renaming a type label would be making the user's product decision for them), the read-side alias 「剥离装备」 stays, and source comments stay. The shipped bundle therefore contains **exactly 3** occurrences of 装备, each named in HANDOFF §3 — counted with `grep -F`, because an earlier `grep -o "2.8.17"` (unescaped dot) manufactured a false positive that the old version string was still in the build.
- **Armor (ruling 3)**: the confirmed 2-for-1 rule is now stated where players read it — the rules page, the inspect panel's 「🛡️护甲」 cell and the same cell in the Codex (both pure display primitives grew an optional `tip`: `gameBoard/uiPrimitives.tsx` `SC`, `Codex.tsx` `StatBox`). The rule itself is unchanged and lives in `core/armorDamage.ts:15`.
- **A wording error I made and corrected inside the same knife**: my first rules-page draft said each general may supply **once per game**. The engine clears `hasSupplied` every turn (`turnEvents.ts:32` `applyTurnActionsResetEvent`, dispatched with the *next* player's id from `TurnResolver.ts:53`). The bug was caught by cross-checking against a glossary row that had been correct all along. Three surfaces (rules page, §二 supply row, §八 item 4) were fixed together. Criterion (§12-76⑥): **one fact is usually written down in more than one place; when you rewrite copy, sweep every place** — and keep the frozen rules table (HANDOFF §4) as the witness, not as something to re-derive.
- **The other four §八 items**: the draw window's 「营地」⇒**「本营」** warning block (four lines; 营地 means the general slots on the board), one deck now has one name —**抽牌堆**— across the draw window, the gate metric label and its hint text, the gate input examples and the drill window's advanced settings (「牌堆≥5」 still parses via `METRIC_ALIASES`; the compounds 看牌堆顶／放回牌堆 deliberately did **not** change, they name the top/bottom of that same pile), 生命⇒体力 ("生命" is now 0 occurrences in source), and `BASE DESTROYED`⇒**本营击破** (line kept, the banner layout starts there).
- **Tests +3 (816 → 819, 78 files)**: `skillExcelFormat.test.ts` "旧词「失去装备牌」读入侧仍认，但绝不写出" (old cell still yields `equipmentLost`; reverse-write contains no 装备; the half-written 「失去装备」 is reported unreadable; the loose entry stays loose) and `battleReport.test.ts` pinning the 叠甲/军备 log line.

## [2.8.17] - 2026-09-29

Ruling #42 from the user's 2026-09-29 batch, now built: **skill prompting gets two modes (智能 / 完整, default 智能) and 「都不发动」 becomes the single exit of the turn-end ask window** — the old 「⏭️ 跳过并结束回合」 button is gone, merged into it exactly as instructed ("回合结束前的「都不发动」和现有「跳过并结束回合」，只留「都不发动」"). The other half of the instruction — *"选择其一那个窗，不给「都不发动」"* — was honoured by **not touching the choice window at all**; its greyed branches from 2.8.11 stay as they were.

- **No new ActionType, on purpose**: 「都不发动」 wires to the existing `skipTurnEndAsk` (`passReaction` + commit the real `END_TURN`). The engine still has no `PASS`/`SKIP`/`DECLINE` action — that was my own over-formalisation in §H9 ("新增一枚 canonical 出口动作"), corrected this round: **when the player decides to do nothing, the only recorded fact is the turn end itself**; a second "I declined" event would record the same thing twice and both would have to reconcile on replay. In-window activations remain canonical `ACTIVATE_SKILL`; the window itself is class-B container observation and is never recorded (D-3c: 窗口可以不录；窗口里的游戏决策不能不录). The right-rail 「⏭️ 结束回合」 while the window is open also means 都不发动 (`gameStore.ts:661`) — it cannot be used to bypass the ask.
- **The structural change that matters: the display list and the legality list are now two views of one derivation**. New `listTurnEndAskItems()` produces `TurnEndAskItem{activatable, disabledReason}` for every self-turn `onTurnEnd` skill on the field, and `listTurnEndSkillCandidates()` was rewritten as `listTurnEndAskItems(...).filter(item => item.activatable)`. Had each mode grown its own query, the AI probe / `legalActions` / validator (which read `candidates`) and the HUD would have drifted apart — the same "display vs. effect" split this project keeps hitting (§12-55 family). Greyed rows therefore **cannot** widen legality by construction; if clicked they still get `SKILL_CONDITION_UNMET` from the resolver with **no ledger write**.
- **What each mode does**: `settings.skillPromptMode` = `'smart'` (default) opens the window only when something is actually activatable and lists only those; `'full'` opens whenever the seat has any `onTurnEnd` skill on the field and lists the blocked ones too, greyed, each with its reason. **Full mode is "ask whenever this kind of skill exists", not "ask every turn"** — a seat with no such skill gets no window in either mode (proved live, see the E2E below). Neither mode changes whether a skill may fire.
- **Reason precedence = the irreversible fact wins** (`disabledReasonOf`): 本回合已发动过 is reported ahead of 不满足发动门槛：…. A gate reads the battlefield *right now* and can become true next action; a spent per-turn ledger entry cannot be undone. Telling the player the reversible one first would point them at a remedy that does not exist.
- **Persisted? No, deliberately**: `skillPromptMode` lives in `settings` next to `autoSave` and is **not written to storage** — it is not a fact about the game and not a replay input, so persisting it would pollute reconstruction. Browser witness after toggling to 完整: `Object.keys(localStorage)` is still just `three_kingdoms_replay_settings`, and that blob contains no `skillPromptMode` key.
- **HUD rework** (`GameBoard.tsx`): each live candidate now also prints its description under the name; blocked candidates render as a non-clickable `<div>` (`✕ 邓艾【屯田】（本回合已发动过）`, `cursor-not-allowed` + grayscale, reason in `title`); a full-mode window where nothing can fire says 「本回合这些技能都不可发动」; and the single exit reads `🚫 都不发动` with the plain-language footnote 「都不发动＝一个也不发动，直接结束本回合」.
- **New tests +12 (804 → 816, 78 files)**: `turnEndSkills.test.ts` +5 (greyed item carries its reason while `candidates` stays empty; ungated skill reports activatable; the ledger takes precedence over the gate text; **single-source assertion** `candidates === items.filter(activatable)` compared with full object equality; `otherTurn` skills invisible in both modes) and `gameStore.turnEndAsk.test.ts` +7 (default is smart; smart + failing gate → no window and the turn commits; full + failing gate → window opens and the turn freezes; activating a greyed entry returns false with zero ledger and the window stays; 都不发动 closes and commits; full mode keeps the window after activation showing both reasons; smart lists only the activatable one).
- **Five gates (final tree)**: `check` 0 errors / `npm test` **816 · 78 files** / coverage **59.77·51.41·50.65·64.91** (Stmts/Branch/Funcs/Lines, floors 42/34/34/47, exit 0, **floors untouched**) / lint **0 errors**, the same 29 legacy warnings, zero new / build **2,033.30 kB · gzip 594.60 kB** = measured **2,033,296** bytes = **+2,534** over v2.8.16 (one greyed render branch plus one settings row); `2.8.17` appears once in the bundle, `2.8.16` zero.
- **Both anchors verbatim — this knife never touched the engine's legality set**: B12 **{"1":106,"2":194}** and B11 **{"1":108,"2":192}**, two runs each on the final tree, `cmp`-identical. Diffed against the v2.8.16 captures after normalisation, **only two lines differ**: the npm echo of the package version and esbuild's bundle-size line (`216.5kb`→`217.2kb`) — neither is game output. Criterion registered as §12-75⑦: **name every surviving diff line and say which layer it lives in, rather than adding a filter pattern** (a new pattern would hide a real drift too).
- **Real-browser E2E** (hotseat room `E2E2817` on dev **:5188**, every click dispatched as a full pointer sequence, one element per evaluate, judgments read from the live `?t=` store module rather than toasts; **no developer mode — this session holds no passphrase**): smart mode at turn 9 opened with **only** the live skill (`⚡ 邓艾【屯田】`) and zero greyed rows, turn frozen at 9; `🚫 都不发动` closed the window and committed (9→10, `turnEndAsk` null, reaction window null); the retired 「跳过并结束回合」 string appears **0 times** in the button list. Full mode then: 玩家1 (no `onTurnEnd` skill on the field) got **no window** and committed 10→11 directly, while 玩家2's turn-11 window showed the live entry — clicking it drew 20→22 cards, wrote ledger `{stableId:'11:jin_007__inst_1:屯田:e1', turn:11, playerId:2}`, left `turn` at 11 (the real END_TURN is still deferred), and the window **stayed open with the row turned grey** (`✕ 邓艾【屯田】（本回合已发动过）`); the second `🚫 都不发动` then committed 11→12. The settings toggle was clicked for real through `⏸️ 菜单` → `游戏设置` → 技能提示 智能⇒完整 (store read `full`, button label reads 完整). **Honest boundary**: the *gate* reason (`不满足发动门槛：体力≥99`) has **no browser witness** — no official or fixture general has a gated `onTurnEnd` skill, and editing skills needs the passphrase. Instead of faking a general, I proved the same render branch through the other reason value; that string's own coverage is the two unit tests. Nothing in this E2E clicked a save/download control.
- **Glossary moved with the UI (rule 4b), and the projection guard was made to bite for real**: §二's 「⏭️ 跳过并结束回合」 entry was rewritten to 「🚫 都不发动」 with the old wording wrapped in `~~…~~` (renders as "（旧说法，已作废）"), 「⏭️ 结束回合」 gained "while the window is open, pressing it again = 都不发动", a new §二 row documents the greyed full-mode row, and §六 documents 技能提示＝智能／完整. Sheet counts §二 37→**38**, §六 17→**18**, total **157→159**; `npm run glossary-xlsx` regenerated `词汇表.xlsx` at **28,034 bytes** (md5 `dd1a8233…`, md `470c583e…`). Bite test this round (a more realistic shape than 2.8.15's fake row): `git checkout` the committed xlsx while keeping the new md → the projection guard fails immediately (`expected 27611 to be 28034`); regenerate → 4/4 green, full 816 green again. Criterion §12-75⑧: **a generated-doc guard should be proven against "forgot to re-run", not just "content mismatch"**.
- **Not in this knife**: #30 决斗 (gameplay knife, three gates), #43 badges + 强制发动 actually doing something (ruling 5 — I owe a short "what does each badge govern" proposal first, and it will most likely move the anchors), the remaining nine §八 wording conflicts plus 装备→军备 and the armor 2-for-1 phrasing (v2.8.18), and the deferred glossary row about the practice-injection default.
- **Registration faces**: HANDOFF §3／§9／**§12-75** (nine criteria: do-nothing is not an action, one derivation for display and legality, what deserves persistence, irreversible reason first, honest substitutes when content is missing, what full mode does *not* mean, name every anchor diff line, prove guards against "forgot to re-run", the todo ledger move), ARCH_MAP **§H9 #42 marked 已落地 with a correction of my own "new canonical action" phrasing** + **§H10 anchor re-verification line**, `PLAYER_GLOSSARY.md` + `词汇表.xlsx`, README, AGENTS.md, both history documents, CHANGELOG.
- **Commit chain, tag, push and remote CI**: feat `596850e` → docs registration `5d1c978` (annotated tag `v2.8.17` on the registration commit) → push → **CI #186** (run `36590505649`, sha `5d1c978`, `event=push` / `head_branch=master`) = **Success**, four jobs green, **0 failed steps** — `lint` 9/9 success, `test (22)` 10/10, `test (24)`'s only non-success step is again the deduplicated coverage upload, `build` 9/9. **Annotations measured per job off the API**: 11 on `lint` = the same 10 react-hooks notices in `GameBoard.tsx` (4 conditional `useEffect` + 5 conditional `useMemo` + 1 missing dependency `cardTypeOrder`) + 1 runner "Node.js 20 is deprecated", and 1 of that same notice on each of the other three jobs ⇒ **14 total, all warnings, 0 errors** — identical distribution to #183/#184, i.e. **zero new**.
- **Push degraded to the proxy tier this round**: direct `curl -I https://github.com` timed out and `git push origin master` died with `Failed to connect to github.com:443 after 21116 ms` ⇒ both master (`bdf61b1..5d1c978`) and the tag (`* [new tag] v2.8.17`) went out through the one-off `-c http.proxy=http://127.0.0.1:10808` invocation; `git config --local/--global http.proxy` re-checked afterwards and **still empty** — no persistent proxy written, per the standing rule.
- **A new environment fact worth reusing (no credentials involved)**: when the remote is unreachable from this machine, the **anonymous** REST reads for a public repo ride the same one-off proxy (`curl -x http://127.0.0.1:10808 https://api.github.com/…`), and all three levels needed for systematic CI verification came back — run status, per-job step conclusions, and `check-runs/{id}/annotations` counts. So browser automation is not the only path to the evidence; nothing here mints a token or touches local git credentials.
- **Two more disproofs collected while listing recent runs**: ① one push carrying **two** commits produced **one** run (bound to the tip sha `5d1c978`, not to `596850e`) — "every commit gets its own CI run" is another misconception; ② pushing the tag produced **no additional run** — the **ninth** positive disproof of "tags trigger CI" (§12-59). Bonus: the previous round's wrap-up commit `bdf61b1` now shows its own run **#185 = completed success**.
- **The backfill commit's own run verified too — CI #187** (run `36591758952`, sha `69d46ca`, **Success**, four jobs green, **0 failed steps**, `test (24)`'s only non-success step again the deduplicated coverage upload; **14 annotations in the same per-job distribution, 0 errors**). All three pushes this round (registration, tag, backfill) rode the **same one-off proxy invocation** — direct `github.com:443` was unreachable from this machine for the whole wrap-up — and no persistent proxy config was written.
- **Relation to the §12-61 "all-grey ⇒ no window (dead-table lock)" rule, stated so nobody reads 2.8.17 as breaking it**: that lock is on the **choice window** (`pendingChoice`), where an all-grey window has no way out other than forcing a branch pick — and the ruling that assigned it to #42 said the explicit exit must ship *with* #42, not be faked by opening a window. The turn-end ask window is structurally different: **it always carries the `END_TURN` exit**, so letting `full` mode open it with every row greyed (plus 「本回合这些技能都不可发动」) *fulfills* that contract. Nothing on the choice side moved — `choiceGates.test.ts`'s "all grey ⇒ no window and no wasted activation" still pins it. Criterion (§12-75⑪): **whether an all-grey state should stop and ask depends on whether that window has an exit, not on how many rows are grey**.

## [2.8.16] - 2026-09-29

The user's ruling from 2026-09-29 about practice batches, now built: **「默认关闭，只有勾选可选项后才打开」** — AI drill runs no longer sprinkle `演練・` practice skills into the general pool unless somebody raises the knob on purpose. This is the knife I flagged as costing a re-anchor (§H9's price tag), so the anchor story is the main content of this entry.

- **Three defaults moved, nothing else**: `defaultMatchConfig().skillInjection` `0.35 → 0` (`src/ai/matchSetup.ts:106`), the CLI's parsed default (`src/ai/battleCli.ts:52`), and the dev dialog's advanced percent (`src/components/AiBattleConfig.tsx:38`), plus the hash fallback in `parseAiBattleHash` (`src/ai/battleHash.ts:94`) so a URL without `skill=` agrees with all three. `PRACTICE_SKILLS` and the injection branch itself are **untouched** — the capability is off by default, not deleted.
- **The restraint that kept one anchor alive**: `matchSetup.ts:265` still reads `if (random() < config.skillInjection)` — I did **not** short-circuit it to `if (config.skillInjection > 0 && …)`. That condition draws an RNG number even at probability 0, and the `--diy-fixture --skill 0` path has always taken that draw; skipping it would have shifted the whole setup stream and **falsely reported B11 as broken**. Criterion: *before turning a switch off, ask whether the switch also consumes randomness — if it does, change the value, never the number of draws.*
- **Re-anchoring, done as renaming rather than re-issuing**: an anchor name encodes **command + pool**, never a reading. The official-pool command's input changed ⇒ **B10 retires at v2.8.15** and the same slot is now **B12**; B11 keeps its name because its *command* still produces the same pool. The whole table is registered as **ARCH_MAP §H10 锚名账本**.
- **New official-pool anchor B12** (`--games 300 --seed 1`, two runs on the version-bumped tree, `cmp`-identical after stripping the timing lines, 93 normalized lines): won=300／exhausted=0／VIOLATIONS=0, winners **{"1":106,"2":194}**; per-faction ledger 魏 108席/58胜·182登场/120阵亡·16攻击/1击杀, 蜀 120/55·186/121·14/1, 吴 134/69·160/111·11/1, 群 127/62·200/123·11/0, 晋 111/56·159/112·6/0 (600 seats, 300 wins).
- **B11 unchanged is the cleanest witness in this entry**: `--diy-fixture --skill 0` still reads **{"1":108,"2":192}** with the 2.8.9 three-faction ledger verbatim ⇒ the change surface is exactly the injection default, nothing else. From now on a non-content knife must match **B12 *and* B11** both verbatim (was B10/B11).
- **One test pinned the old default, and only one**: `src/ai/battleReport.test.ts:239` asserted `skill: 0.35` out of `parseAiBattleHash` — changed to `0`. Criterion: *before changing a default, ask which test will go red; the answer is the repo's own list of places that treat the default as a fact.*
- **New tests +3 (801 → 804, all in `src/ai/matchSetup.test.ts`)**: the default reads 0; four seeds (`1, 7, 33, 202`) each assemble a pool containing **zero** `演練・` skills; and `skillInjection: 1` still yields exactly 12 injected (2 players × 6 pool) — the closed default and the still-working capability both asserted, because "off by default" and "removed" must never be allowed to converge silently.
- **Real-browser E2E, both directions** (dev **:5188**, player state, `#ai-battle` route with `?t=` instances): a URL with **no `skill=`** shows the config line 「技能注入 **0%**」, 10 games all reach a verdict, and the string 「演練」 appears **0 times** in the whole log; a URL **with `skill=0.35`** reads 「技能注入 **35%**」 and completes 6 games — which is the point: the default closed, the capability did not. **Boundary stated honestly**: the dialog itself is gated behind developer mode (`MainMenu.tsx:107`), so this session reached it by direct URL, not by the menu; I hold no passphrase.
- **Follow-on impact registered, not silently inherited**: `src/ai/arena.ts` and the §G 500-game content audit both start from the CLI/config defaults, so any future run that wants to compare against pre-2.8.16 readings must pass `--skill 0.35` **explicitly** (§H10). An anchor command must never depend on a default — `--skill 0` is now redundant but still spelled out for exactly that reason.
- **A WPS environment fact, found the hard way**: I edited the glossary row for the new default, then `npm run glossary-xlsx` died with **`EBUSY: resource busy or locked, open '…词汇表.xlsx'`** — the user has that workbook open in WPS. Rather than leave the md and its committed projection divergent (the 2.8.15 guard would turn `npm test` red), I **reverted the md row** to `bc16c87d…` and re-ran the scripts guards (9 green). Criterion: *when a generated artifact is locked by the user's editor, keep the upstream source self-consistent and come back — don't half-apply.* `.gitignore` gained `~$*` because WPS's owner file `~$词汇表.xlsx` really did appear untracked this round.
- **Five gates (final tree)**: `check` 0 errors / `npm test` **804 · 78 files** (+3) / coverage **two readings** 59.57·51.20·50.36·64.71 and 59.59·51.16·50.46·64.70 against floors 42/34/34/47 (exit 0, **floors unchanged**) / lint **0 errors**, the same 29 legacy warnings, zero new / build **2,030.76 kB · gzip 593.82 kB** = measured **2,030,762** bytes = **+22** over v2.8.15 (the longer dialog hint against two shorter numeric defaults); `2.8.16` appears once in the bundle, `2.8.15` zero.
- **A disclosed judgment call (代决)**: the user's words were "只有勾选可选项后才打开" — literally a checkbox. What shipped is a percentage defaulting to 0, because the knob already exists as a percent and a bool would have meant a second control path. The effect matches the sentence (off unless you ask), and swapping it for a literal `practice=on` toggle later is a UI-only change with no engine or anchor consequences. Registered as such in §H10 rather than presented as if it were the literal ask.
