## [2.8.15] - 2026-09-29

The user asked for one thing by name: turn the player glossary into a spreadsheet called 词汇表. Delivered as **`词汇表.xlsx` at the repo root** — but as a *projection* of `PLAYER_GLOSSARY.md`, not a second copy: the md stays the single source of truth and `npm run glossary-xlsx` regenerates the workbook from it. **Zero `src/` changes** ⇒ non-gameplay cut ⇒ both anchors had to match verbatim.

- **What is generated**: 9 sheets — `说明` (title + md preamble + a per-sheet row count) plus one sheet per glossary section (`一 棋盘与数字` 30 ／ `二 行动按钮与提示` 37 ／ `三 抽卡与开局` 21 ／ `四 技能相关词` 25 ／ `五 结算录像日志` 9 ／ `六 图鉴设置开发者` 17 ／ `七 界面里没有的词` 8 ／ `八 措辞与规则冲突` 10 = **157 terms**). Frozen header row, per-section column widths, italic footnote for §四's "the badges are not switches" paragraph, and every markdown mark stripped (`**`, backticks, table separators; `~~old wording~~` becomes "old wording（旧说法，已作废）"). Sheet names respect Excel's limits (≤31 chars, no `: \ / ? * [ ]`).
- **The real bug this cut found — a committed binary must not churn**: two consecutive runs over the same md produced **different bytes** (`4df24a07…` vs `2be9264f…`). Pinning `workbook.created/modified` was not enough: exceljs hands every zip entry to jszip, which stamps `new Date()` per entry. Fix = repack the archive through jszip with all entry timestamps pinned to one fixed UTC date. Evidence: three runs in a row now give the same md5, `unzip -l` shows every entry at `2026-09-29 00:00`, and a guard test asserts "two renders are byte-identical" permanently. Criterion: **a generated file checked into the repo should only diff when its content changed** — otherwise the history can no longer answer "what did this version of the glossary actually change".
- **Guard test `scripts/make-glossary-xlsx.test.mjs` (4 cases), read back through the *other* Excel library**: the project's own round-trip rule is "write with ExcelJS, read with SheetJS", so the test uses SheetJS — a generator never grades its own homework. The four: sheet names legal; every md row lands cell-for-cell (plus §四's footnote, plus **zero markdown residue**); two renders byte-identical; **the committed `词汇表.xlsx` equals the current md's projection**. Its bite was proven, not assumed: appending one fake row to the md flipped the fourth case red (27,486 vs 27,545 bytes); after reverting, all four pass and the md checksum is back to `5abd2728…`. That was the pre-final tree — the "您的理解→你的理解" wording fix below then changed the md once more, so the **final** figures are md `bc16c87d…` and `词汇表.xlsx` 27,611 bytes (`980ec7d3…`), regenerated and re-guarded (4/4 green).
- **One pre-existing flake, proven pre-existing before being touched**: `vitest.config.ts` never set `testTimeout`, so the 5s default killed three heavy cases on a cold local run (`ai/arena`, `core/gameFlow`, `ai/policies/strategyPolicy`). With all of this round's work stashed (`git stash -u`) the clean **v2.8.14** tree reproduced the identical timeout in one of six coverage runs ⇒ not introduced here ⇒ raised to 20s. No test and no coverage floor was touched. Note the coverage `include` is `src/**` only, so tests under `scripts/` never move the floor readings.
- **No `.gitattributes`, deliberately**: the existing `skills-sample.xlsx` fixture is already stored as `i/-text` (git detects the NUL bytes, so `core.autocrlf=true` never touches it) and the projection guard would turn red if a binary ever did arrive corrupted. Adding a repo-wide attribute file to solve a problem that measures as absent is scope creep.
- **Glossary header updated**: `PLAYER_GLOSSARY.md` now states that the md is the source of truth, that the Excel's third column 你的理解 is the one for the user to fill, and — the honest cost of a projection — **answers written into the Excel must be copied back into the md**, because regenerating overwrites the workbook. Also corrected my own new wording ("您的理解" → the column's actual name "你的理解").
- **Five gates (final tree)**: `check` 0 errors / `npm test` **801 · 78 files** (+4, +1 file = the new guard) / coverage **three readings**: 59.72·51.28·50.56·64.86, 59.59·51.15·50.51·64.70, 59.60·51.20·50.51·64.71 against floors 42/34/34/47 (exit 0 all three, **floors unchanged**) / lint **0 errors**, the same 29 legacy warnings, zero new / build **2,030.74 kB · gzip 593.81 kB** = measured **2,030,740** bytes = **the same byte count as v2.8.14** (`2.8.15` appears once, `2.8.14` zero) — the structural witness that no runtime code changed.
- **Both anchors verbatim, two runs each on the version-bumped tree** (`cmp` after stripping the timing lines): B10 97 ≡ 97 lines → won=300／exhausted=0／VIOLATIONS=0, winners **{"1":112,"2":188}**, five-faction ledger matching §12-43① cell for cell; B11 (`--diy-fixture --skill 0`) 13 ≡ 13 lines → **{"1":108,"2":192}**, three-faction ledger identical to the 2.8.9 registration.
- **What was NOT done, stated plainly**: no browser E2E this round — `src/` is untouched and the bundle is byte-identical, so there was no interface behaviour to verify. And **I cannot confirm what the workbook looks like when opened in real Excel** (no Excel on this machine); my witnesses are the cross-library read-back and zip integrity, nothing more. Opening it is the user's click.
- **Registration faces**: HANDOFF §3／§9／§12-73 (four standing criteria: projection not copy ／ generated binaries must be byte-stable ／ judge with the other library ／ prove the guard bites), ARCH_MAP glossary row + §12 pointer, README document list, AGENTS.md (verification command + key-file row), both history documents, CHANGELOG.
- **Commit chain, tag, push and remote CI (backfilled)**: feat `9a36f6b` → docs registration `6faa1b8` → docs self-correction `b503c71`, with the annotated tag `v2.8.15` **re-pointed to `b503c71`** while it was still local-only (never pushed, so nobody could have fetched the old one; `git rev-parse v2.8.15^{}` = `b503c71`). Direct push failed (`Failed to connect to github.com:443 after 21059 ms`) while the local proxy port was listening, so master and the tag each went out through a **one-off** `git -c http.proxy=…` push; afterwards `http.proxy` was re-checked empty at `--local`/`--global` and nothing proxy-shaped at `--system` or in the environment. **CI #180** (run `36571087589`, sha `b503c71`) = **Success**, four jobs green, **0 failed steps** (`lint` 98s · `test (22)` 133s incl. the coverage-floored run · `test (24)` 63s, its only non-success step the deduplicated coverage upload · `build` 47s). Annotations were counted **per job off the API rather than copied from last round**: 11 on `lint` (10 legacy react-hooks + 1 runner "Node.js 20 is deprecated" notice) and 1 of that same deprecation notice on each of the other three jobs ⇒ **14 total, all warnings, 0 errors**. Two record-keeping corrections follow from that measurement: the v2.8.14 entry credited all 11 `lint` annotations to react-hooks, and the 10 CI-side hook annotations are *not* the same number as the 29 warnings local eslint reports (GitHub returns only part per check run). The remote tree at that sha carries `scripts/make-glossary-xlsx.test.mjs` (3,506 B) and `make-glossary-xlsx.mjs` (5,522 B), so the projection guard really executed on both matrix legs; and the API reports `词汇表.xlsx` as **27,611 bytes remotely = the local byte size**, a live check of the "no `.gitattributes` needed" call. Pushing the tag again produced **no additional run** — the **seventh** positive disproof of "tags trigger CI" (§12-59). Per the v2.8.5–v2.8.13 precedent the wrap-up commit's own run is not chased — there is no "CI of the CI".

## [2.8.14] - 2026-09-29

Half of the user's six rulings from 2026-09-29 — **only the two that do not touch gameplay**: the skill-trigger report can no longer miss an effect type, and the rules page finally says who draws the compensation card. The other four rulings are frozen as buildable wording in **ARCH_MAP §H9** (duel ×4, hint modes, #38/#39/#41). Non-content cut ⇒ **both anchors must match verbatim**.

- **What the user actually asked for (#39)**: "修复报告的统计名单以后不会漏收就好" — *the report's roster must never under-collect again*. That is not a request for a longer list; it is a request for a mechanism. Ground truth found **two** distortions, not one.
- **Distortion 1 — the filter**: `battleRunner` carried a hand-maintained set of event types that had already drifted **three times** (GIVE in 2.5.3, EQUIP_STRIP in 2.6.0, REVEAL／DECK_PLACE in 2.6.1 — each landed in the bridge and nobody remembered the list). Replaced by `isSkillEffectEvent(ev)`, a **producer-side predicate**: `SkillTriggerBridge.translateEffect` (`src/skills/SkillTriggerBridge.ts:265-273`) stamps `skillId` **and** `effectType` into every effect event it mints and nowhere else in the codebase, so a new effect type joins the report *by construction, not by memory*.
- **Distortion 2 — the roster**: `battleCli --skill-stats` derived its expected rows from the official ledger unconditionally (39 rows), so a `--diy-fixture` batch listed those 39 as zeros and reported all twelve `D-fix-*` samples as off-list. **Counts had been right all along; the name list was wrong** (this closes the observation-layer caveat registered as §12-58). Now `configuredSkillRows(generalsForPoolSource(...))` — *filter and roster are the same layer and must share one source*.
- **CLI evidence, taken from real runs, not inferred**: fixed tree, `--diy-fixture --skill 0 --skill-stats --games 40` → 「配置技能 **12** 条 · 触发过 **9** 条 · 零触发 3 条」 with `样·归整(DECK_PLACE)=3` and `样·探报(REVEAL)=2`; before the fix the same mode read "39 条 · 触发过 0 条". Official pool still lists 39 rows, and its off-list entries are the default-0.35 **practice-skill injection**, which is existing semantics, not a miss.
- **Compensation draw (`src/components`, one line)**: the user ruled **「归阵亡方」**. The engine was already right — `chainedConsequences.ts:135-152` queues `DRAW_REQUIRED` for `targetPlayerId`, pinned by `core/EventProcessor.test.ts:330` — so the *only* thing wrong was the rules page. `Rules.tsx:38` now reads 「将领被击破后，由失去这名将领的一方补抽 1 张」. Criterion: **when the wording and the resolution disagree, first establish which side is authoritative — do not default to changing code to chase prose.**
- **`PLAYER_GLOSSARY.md` reconciliation**: the §三 term no longer claims "两处相反"; the §八 conflict table row 1 is filled in (✅ 归阵亡方, both sides now agree) with the old sentence struck through; §七's 决斗 row now states the rules *were* given in full (§H5-6 plus the four 2026-09-29 answers) and only the implementation is outstanding — correcting a claim this session had wrongly registered as "rules still missing".
- **ARCH_MAP §H9 (paper)**: the duel's four questions and answers hung under §H5-6 as the same flow (skills-only initiator ／ no hand cost per strike because it is not a real melee attack ／ death in a duel counts as 击破 ／ damage type = skill damage), the hint-mode contract (**both 完整 and 智能 share one explicit 「都不发动」 canonical exit, default 智能**), and the disposition of #38／#39／#41 — including the **price tag on #41**: the injection probability lives inside B10's RNG consumption chain, so flipping its default means **re-anchoring**, not editing a number.

## [2.8.13] - 2026-09-29

Closing cut for **#49** plus one surface the user asked for by name — **the number-`0` sentinel now means "all" only where the engine can actually read a hand, and every import now leaves a permanent, copyable roll-up of what it wrote** (`src/components` + `src/skills` + one store return value; not a content cut ⇒ both anchors must stay verbatim, and they do). New player-facing document: `PLAYER_GLOSSARY.md`.

- **User rulings taken verbatim as acceptance criteria (2026-09-29)**: GUI option ① — the "all" affordance appears **only** for the three types that read a hand; **B** — on the Excel side, `0` is accepted **per type**, every other type's `0` is treated as *blank* **and named in the receipt**; **option 2** — the spreadsheet also accepts the word 「全部」 as an alias, while **export keeps writing `0`** so the round-trip shape stays single.
- **GUI half (`RuntimeEditor`)**: a 「整只手（全部）」 checkbox rendered only for `WHOLE_HAND_RUNTIME_TYPES` (弃牌／发放／放回牌堆); when checked, **the number box is not rendered at all** — showing `1` next to an active "all" is exactly the display/effect split §12-55 warns about; `handleTypeChange` applies `Math.max(1, carried)` when moving to a type that does not understand `0`, so **the sentinel can no longer be smuggled across types**; preview strings became whole sentences (「弃全部手牌」, not 「弃 全部 张手牌」).
- **Excel half (`readValueCell(raw, type)`)**: for the three types, `0`／`全部`／the GUI label all mean `value: 0`; for the other six, a `0` or 「全部」 means **this cell is blank** and produces a `valueNote` naming the general, the skill, the effect slot and why — including the engine's own words ("引擎会把它当成 1"). **`REVEAL` is singled out**: it is the one observer using `Math.max(0, …)`, so its `0` is not clamped and really means 「看 0 张，一张也不看」. Notes ride the **existing `parseWarnings` channel** (the persistent list), never a toast.
- **Aliases go in, never out**: `serializeEffectGroup` was not touched — it still writes `0`. Accepting a friendly word on read while emitting the canonical shape on write is what keeps "re-import my own file" a no-op, which is last round's invariant #2.
- **Import summary panel (the user's named request, three rulings implemented as stated)**: ① **one shared summary for both import paths** (text and Excel, including deferred create/modify and batch create); ② a **persistent panel** with a 「知道了」 button and a 复制全文 button (clipboard API, `execCommand` fallback; what the panel shows and what gets copied are the same string); ③ **naming only** — which skills were added/removed/changed, which of 势力/体力/近战/远程 moved, never flattened into slot counts.
- **How that summary is derived (the one place this cut could have silently produced an empty panel)**: every entry comes from **taking a snapshot of the currently effective view before the write and again after it** — the same口径 #47 settled ("a change is A→B in the effective view", not "diff against the repo card"), and the only legitimate source is `store.getGeneralWithEdits`, so the component does not keep a second merge implementation. It **must re-read `useGameStore.getState()`**: the write lands synchronously inside the same event handler, while the closure's selector still points at the previous render — using the stale one makes before ≡ after and **every change entry vanishes without an error** (hit for real this round; now pinned by comment and test).
- **The store reports names, not wording**: `importSkillEditsFromText` returns an added `applied: string[]` of general ids it actually wrote; the vocabulary stays in the editor layer and the store emits no user-facing sentence (three existing assertions updated with the signature).
- **Same-name skills are no longer double-reported**: `describeSkillChanges` compares per *distinct name* by count, because walking pairwise repeats "新增技能 X" once for every later same-named skill and prints the same line twice; identical sets in a different order honestly say 「技能顺序调整」.
- **`PLAYER_GLOSSARY.md` (new, player-facing)**: eight sections, ~149 rows, three columns (term ／ plain-language translation ／ a blank column for the user's own reading). §七 lists words the UI **does not** have (杀/闪/桃/酒/决斗/锦囊/判定/装备区/马/淘汰 …), §八 lists **ten places where the on-screen wording and the rules or the engine disagree**, each with a code citation — two of them found while writing it: the compensation draw after a general falls goes to **the dead general's own side** (`chainedConsequences.ts:135-152` uses `targetPlayerId`) while the rules page says 「击破方补抽」 (`Rules.tsx:38`), and **技能标签／强制发动 still have zero consumers** in the compiler and bridge (the §12-61 finding, now visible to players).
- **Tests 749/74 → 794/77 (+45 / +3 files)**: new `importSummary.test.ts` 12, `SkillEditor.importSummary.test.tsx` 9, `RuntimeEditor.wholeHand.test.tsx` 15; `skillExcelFormat.test.ts` +6 (three types accept `0`／「全部」／GUI label; the other five say "按没填处理"＋"当成 1"; `REVEAL` says "看 0 张" and must *not* say "当成 1"; all nine alias forms are read-only; positives／`无`／text／negatives／no-type unchanged; whole-group `GIVE 全部`→`value:0` and `REVEAL 0`→no value＋note), `skillExcelParsers.test.ts` +3 (弃牌＋全部 ⇒ clean import, zero warnings; 摸牌＋0 ⇒ exactly one warning naming general·skill·slot; 看牌堆顶＋0 ⇒ warns 看 0 张); the #48 round-trip pin additionally asserts `valueNote` is absent for 断肠.
- **Differential witnesses (four sabotage experiments, each biting only its own cases, each reverted byte-for-byte from a backup followed by a full 794-case re-run)**: (A) drop the type gate on the numeric `0` inside `readValueCell` (`if (type && WHOLE_HAND_RUNTIME_TYPES.includes(type))` → `if (type)`) ⇒ **exactly 4 failures** (two in the format file, two in the parser file, including the `REVEAL` one). (B) drop the gate on the **word** 「全部」 instead ⇒ **exactly 1 failure** — the two gates are pinned separately, they are not two faces of one generic check. (C) set `supportsWholeHand = !!runtime` in `RuntimeEditor` ⇒ **exactly 6 failures**, the six `it.each` "不给勾选框" cases (which is also why that file holds 15 cases, not 6). (D) make the exporter write 「全部」 for `value === 0` ⇒ **exactly 2 failures** — 断肠's round trip and the "aliases go in, never out" pin.

## [2.8.12] - 2026-09-29

Fix cut from the user's own live feedback — **the Excel round trip stopped lying about what it did** (three findings: #45 no-change skip, #46 wording that follows the actual ownership rule, #48 the value-`0` fidelity bug found *by* that round trip; `src/components` + `src/skills` only; not a content cut ⇒ both anchors must stay verbatim, and they do).

- **#45 — re-importing an unchanged file no longer counts as an import, and no longer leaves 95 empty ✏️ overlays.** Of the three import format branches, **only the simple one** ever asked "would writing this row actually change anything"; the row-per-skill format (which is exactly what `handleExport` produces) and the legacy detailed format did not, so re-feeding the app's own workbook wrote one empty overlay per official general — and empty overlays are not harmless, because the ✏️ badges and the 「🚫 N 处改动已停用」 banner both read those same two tables. New `importEntryChangesNothing(edited, gEdit, skills)` is checked on both paths (`SkillEditor.tsx:345`, `:367`); a no-change row is counted under 「跳过 N 名无变化」 and **writes not one byte**.
- **Two rulings inside that check, both written down because they are easy to "fix" back into a bug**: ① it compares against the **currently effective view** (`getEditedGeneral`), because the question a skip answers is "would this write change anything", not "does the file match the ledger" — the consequence is registered as open decision **#47**, not silently repaired (see the next entry's test); ② **permission comes first**: `mayEditGeneral(id) && …`, so a row the player is not allowed to write still lands in the refusal list — *"no change" must never overwrite the truth "I could not write this"*.
- **The hard part of that check is normalisation**, and it is the whole difficulty: the effect `id` is regenerated on every parse (`e${Date.now()}_n`) and must be **excluded**; `''` ⇔ `undefined` and `forced:false` ⇔ `undefined` differ between the store side and the parser side; and export writes 「全部生效」 whenever effects exist and `effectMode` is unset, so both sides read that as `'all'`. Compare value tuples, never raw objects.
- **#46 — wording follows the mode; the ownership rule was not touched (user chose option A).** The pending-import panel promised 「新建为 DIY 将领」 while developer mode really creates an **official local draft** (`G-*`). Three places now share one derived string `pendingCreateLayer = developerMode ? '官方本地草稿' : 'DIY 将领'` (panel line, single-create toast, batch-create toast); **`developerMode ? 'official' : 'DIY'` is byte-for-byte unchanged**, because which layer a card lands in is §H1's rule, not this cut's business. Standing lesson: *any sentence that predicts which layer something lands in must be derived from the same expression that decides it.*
- **#48 — the real damage of this round, found only by actually doing the round trip in the browser.** Export uses `exceljs`, import uses SheetJS, and the two sides disagreed about **`0`**: the exporter writes `rt.value != null ? rt.value : 无`, i.e. **`0` verbatim**, while `parseRuntimeValue` rejected `n < 1` ⇒ the importer read `undefined` ⇒ the consumer `SkillTriggerBridge.ts:341` (`Number(effect.value ?? 1)`) turned it into **1**. `value: 0` is the engine's **「all」 sentinel** (`handSelection.ts:42` `count === 0 ? hand.length : …`) and an official card actually uses it — 蔡文姬·断肠, 「the killer discards **all** their cards」 (`data/generals.ts:675`; exactly **1** of the 40 official runtime effects, verified by `grep -c "value: 0"`). So the user, having done nothing but back up their own spreadsheet and re-import it, would have quietly turned 断肠 into 「discard **1** card」 and stamped it ✏️. Fix ＝ **accept 0**; negative, fractional and text cells still return `undefined`, nothing else was loosened.
- **New standing invariant**: `value: 0` is a meaningful legal value, not an empty cell. Whatever the exporter writes, the importer must read back verbatim — rejecting a sentinel because it "looks unfilled" is worst not when it errors but when it **silently becomes a different number**. Any future sentinel (`0` / `-1` / empty string) must be changed in exporter + importer + editor together, with one round-trip test pinning it.
- **Tests 730/73 → 749/74 (+19 / +1 file)**: new `SkillEditor.importIdempotent.test.tsx` (7 cases — unchanged official card re-imports as skip with zero overlay records; a genuinely edited row still writes and the second identical import adds nothing to the save; a file that writes back the original hp is judged "no change" rather than pretending success [#47's characterization pin]; **export the whole book and re-import it verbatim ⇒ zero writes, receipt says skip only** [the #45+#48 full-corpus case, with `file-saver` intercepted and counted — a real anchor click is never let through]; **the same unchanged official row under *player* mode must report 拒录, never 无变化** — the permission-first pin the third gate proved was missing; plus both #46 modes asserting the `G-*`/`D-*` prefixes) — `skillExcelParsers.test.ts` +9, `skillExcelFormat.test.ts` +3 (0 reads back as 0, 断肠 group round trip `{type:'DISCARD',value:0,target:'ATTACKER'}`, `-1`/`abc`/`无` still `undefined`).
- **Differential witness (so the new tests cannot be vacuous)**: four independent sabotage experiments, each with its own predicted victim. (A) flip `n < 0` back to `n < 1` and the full-corpus case fails exactly where predicted — `expected '✅ 从 1 个工作表导入 1 名将领，跳过 94 名无变化' to contain '导入 0 名将领'`; restore the fix and it is green. The 94→95 shift is precisely the moment 断肠 becomes a no-change row. (B) disable the two skip call sites ⇒ the #45 cases go red. (C) hardcode `pendingCreateLayer` to one of the two strings ⇒ the other mode's #46 test goes red. (E) **delete the `mayEditGeneral(id) &&` prefix** ⇒ **only** the new player-mode pin turns red, with `expected '✅ 从 0 个工作表导入 0 名将领，跳过 1 名无变化' to contain '拒录 1 名'` — that experiment is what proves the permission-first branch is a real decision and not decoration.
- **Five gates (final tree, after the third gate's finding was folded in)**: `check` 0 errors / `npm test` **749 · 74 files** / coverage **58.88 / 50.42 / 49.28 / 64.19** over floors 42/34/34/47 (exit 0, **floors unchanged**; readings on intermediate trees — 58.91/50.46/49.02/64.26, 58.93/50.51/49.07/64.28 — differ only by the §12-22④ snapshot-jitter口径 plus the extra test's lines, not an anchor) / `lint` **0 errors**, 29 legacy warnings, same four categories, zero new / `build` single file **2,022.83 kB · gzip 591.47 kB = 2,022,829 bytes** (vs 2.8.11's 2,021.74/591.22 = +1.09/+0.25 kB, the size of a skip check plus normalisation plus strings; **byte-identical before and after the last test was added** — tests do not enter the artifact, which is the structural witness that the final tree's runtime code did not change).
- **Both anchors verbatim, two runs each**: B10 `npm run ai-battle -- --games 300 --seed 1` → won=300 / exhausted=0 / VIOLATIONS=0, winners **{"1":112,"2":188}**, five-faction ledger identical to §12-43① cell by cell, two runs equal except the timing line; B11 `… --diy-fixture --skill 0` → **{"1":108,"2":192}** with the three-faction ledger (Wei 217/121·339/197·33/2, Shu 193/87·275/186·12/1, Wu 190/92·297/214·8/1) and the fixture trigger readings (蓄粮 42 / 双略 32 / 回报 7 / 转赠 6 / 固守 4 / 节用 4 / 抚伤 3 / 焚粮 3 / 缴械 2) identical to the v2.8.9 registration.
- **Real-browser E2E** (dev 5173, from a clean machine state — all six `three_kingdoms_*` keys `"[]"`; developer mode entered once through the browser field): 📤 export produced a 38,930-byte workbook, which was fed back through the page's real `<input type=file>` ⇒ receipt 「导入 **0** 名将领，跳过 **95** 名无变化」, both edit keys stayed `"[]"` for the whole trip, and **no ✏️ appeared anywhere** (#45 and #48 close in the same breath); the same session read the #46 panel live as 「新建为 **官方本地草稿**」. Housekeeping: editor closed, page reloaded (exiting developer mode needs no passphrase), six keys re-checked as `"[]"`.
- **An evidence-method correction for this project, recorded at §12-66④**: my first live readings ("1 imported", "wrote a `G-imp_*` draft") came from reading the **DOM toast**, which expires after 5 s, gets captured stale by a `MutationObserver`, and survives HMR remounts that clear the file input. From then on every "did it write?" assertion reads **store + localStorage**, and the toast is only used to check what the user actually sees.
- **Third gate (independent recompute) verdict = fit to register**, with **two of its own claims disproved by evidence before anything was copied into the tree**: 「the exporter writes 0 as 无」 is false (HEAD has no lower-bound guard, and the round-trip test asserting `cells[3] === 0` has been green all along), and "8 official skills use 0" is really **1**. It also returned the one *valid* finding of this knife: the permission-first branch was structurally correct but **unpinned** — every existing test happened to pass with the `mayEditGeneral` prefix removed. That gap was closed inside this cut (the player-mode pin plus witness (E) above), and all four gates re-run on the resulting tree. The session additionally exceeded its read-only brief by implementing #49 in a temporary worktree — **not adopted**, since that half of it decides an open user question; the tree was reconciled against the pre-registration baseline by `git status`/`git diff --stat` and contains only this session's seven files. Durable rule: *a recompute session's deliverable is its conclusions, never its diffs.*
- **Deliberately not done by this cut**: **#47** (field diffs are computed against the repo original, so writing the original value back cannot remove an existing overlay — pinned as a characterization test instead of silently re-spec'd) and **#49** (`RuntimeEditor.tsx:59-60` has `min={1}` + `Math.max(1, …)`, so saving 断肠 from the editor still clamps 0 to 1 — the GUI half of the same defect; what is owed is a ruling on **how the input surface should say "all"**, and whether the number box may hold 0 at all, which is product wording, not a fix). #38/#39/#41/#42/#43 unchanged.
- **Registration faces**: ARCH_MAP §F (new subsection 「Excel 往返保真与「无变化」跳过」) + §H8 (a v2.8.12 landed-status line), HANDOFF §3 / §9 / §12-65~67, CHANGELOG, README test line, AGENTS.md (Excel round-trip bullet + a new standing rule), both history documents.
- **Remote CI = green, verified to step level**: **CI #170** (run `36533512701`, sha `d422652` — the registration commit, `event=push` / `head_branch=master`), **Success**, total **2m35s**. The run page reads **Test Files ✅ 74 / Test Results ✅ 749** on each matrix leg, and the public API confirms every step conclusion: `lint` (Security audit + Run ESLint all success), `test (22)` (type check + tests-with-coverage + coverage upload success), `test (24)` (same, with `Upload coverage report = skipped` = matrix dedup, not a failure), `build` (bundle + artifact upload success) ⇒ **0 failed steps**; annotations = **14 = 11 legacy react-hooks warnings + 3 Node.js-20 deprecation notices, 0 errors**. The feat commit `10e5d7a` starts no run of its own (a push runs only its head sha) and is covered by `d422652`'s 749. Previous head `8bf6be7` (v2.8.11 wrap-up) = **CI #169**, also Success. **The annotated tag `v2.8.12` was pushed too and still produced exactly one run** — the fourth positive disproof of "pushing a tag triggers CI" (§12-59). The backfill commit `a775a05` was then verified in-session too: it was **enqueued twice at the same second** (**CI #171** run `36534277571` and **#172** run `36534278124`, same sha, both `head_branch=master`, unrelated to tags — the second recorded instance of §12-59's "two runs on one sha = duplicate enqueue"), **both Success** (2m34s / 2m40s), four jobs green, 0 failed steps, each summarising **Test Files ✅ 74 / Test Results ✅ 749**; that push went through directly, no proxy needed.

## [2.8.11] - 2026-09-29

Skill knife #23 — **"choose one of" branches can now carry gates, and a branch that fails its gate is greyed with the reason visible instead of hidden** (ARCH_MAP §F on-demand list item 9 closed; a *gameplay* cut ⇒ three-gate protocol ① and ③ both fired; both anchors still verbatim because nothing shipped carries a gate).

- **The four user rulings of 2026-09-29 are the acceptance criteria**, quoted in spirit: ① "skill information is meant to be fully public to the player — there is no such thing as leaking it by showing it" ⇒ greyed + a plain-language reason, never hidden; ③ "evaluate the group gate first, then the per-option gates"; ④ "add a column to Excel"; plus the shape ruling that a gated choice group still opens its window (option A) rather than filtering the list silently.
- **Two levels, two homes — which is why the required order costs no ordering code**: the **group gate** lives on the skill (`Skill.conditions`, new optional field in `src/data/generals.ts`) and compiles onto the choice definition's single `conditions` slot, evaluated by `SkillTriggerBridge.buildCondition` **before any option is built** — fail it and no option exists at that moment, so per-option gates never participate; the **per-option gate** lives on each effect (`SkillEffectData.conditions`) and is evaluated branch by branch in `buildChoiceOptions`. Single-effect definitions merge both into one slot via `mergeGates()` (AND, group first; both empty ⇒ `undefined`, so a gate-free skill compiles byte-identically to v2.8.10).
- **What was revoked**: `CONDITION_CHOICE_UNSUPPORTED` — v2.8.3's "one `conditions` slot cannot express per-effect gates, so withhold the whole group" — is deleted from `SkillSkip.reason`, together with the two v2.8.3 test pins that asserted it (the ledger therefore reads **+21 net** = 23 added − 2 revoked, 709 → 730) and the editor's red warning "选择其一暂时不能配门槛". CHANGELOG 2.8.3 line 135 and PROJECT_HISTORY_AI's 2.8.3 section still carry the old ruling **on purpose**: dated entries record what was believed then; this entry supersedes them.
- **Grey ≠ deleted, and legality has exactly one source of truth**: `PendingChoiceOption` gains optional `enabled`/`gateText`, written **only on failing branches** (label and events are kept, and a gate-free offer has neither key, so the replay shape is backward compatible); `GameBoard.tsx` renders it disabled, greyscaled, prefixed `✕`, with 「（不满足：手牌≥9）」 inline and the reason in `title`. `rules/legalActions.ts` skips greyed options when enumerating `CHOOSE_OPTION` (so probe / AI driver / validator / UI all read the same fact), and `ChooseOptionResolver` answers `CHOICE_OPTION_LOCKED` **from the frozen state** — it never re-reads the world at decision time, consistent with choice's deferred-settlement contract (evaluation happens once, when the window opens, and is frozen into the recorded offer).
- **Two honest-failure paths, which were the real risk of this cut**: an **all-grey offer never opens** (an offer with nothing selectable freezes the table — same contract as the existing "empty candidate set ⇒ no offer"), and `TurnEndSkillResolver` then returns `SKILL_CONDITION_UNMET` **without writing the activation ledger**, otherwise one click would consume an activation for "fired, nothing happened". The AI driver also had to change shape: `state.chooseOption(0)` rested on "an in-range index is always legal while the offer is live", which gates break (hard-clicking a greyed option gets rejected ⇒ zero progress ⇒ infinite loop); it now takes the first `enabled !== false` option and does nothing at all if none exists, leaving it to the stall guard.
- **Excel: exactly one new column, and the correction owed to the user.** The new fixed column is **「技能门槛」** (group gate, 0-based index 11). The per-effect gate column is **not** new — it has existed since v2.8.3 as the 7th column of each effect group; my earlier requirement restatement just said "add a column" without that distinction, and §12-62 records the correction. Because inserting a fixed column shifts every effect group, the group start is now **read from the header** (`detectEffectGroupStart`, matches `^效果1标注$`, falls back to 11) ⇒ the user's older 11-fixed-column files import unchanged and mean "no group gate" (pinned by a dedicated regression case); dropdown-validation columns, width array and comment positions all moved in the same step (§12-46⑤), and an unreadable group-gate clause is reported line by line into the existing `parseWarnings` channel — never dropped.
- **Persistence is zero-migration**: `SkillEdit` / `skillEdits` gained an optional `conditions?`, so an older localStorage entry simply means "no gate"; the editor's "switch to single-effect mode" now also clears the group gate, with the reason written into the button's tooltip — **no gate that the player cannot see**.
- **Five gates (final tree, i.e. after the third-gate recompute fixes below)**: `check` 0 errors / `npm test` **730 · 73 files** (709/72 → 730/73; new `src/skills/choiceGates.test.ts` 8 cases covering grey-with-reason, "grey is not a legal action and clicking it is rejected", live branch still settles, all-grey ⇒ no window and no wasted activation, gate-free group shape identical to v2.8.10, group-gate-fails-first, plus two mixed cases; 4 compiler cases, 4 Excel **import**-side cases, **4 Excel export-side cases added by the recompute**, 1 editor case, 2 driver cases) / coverage **56.72/48.66/47.76/61.85** over floors 42/34/34/47 (exit 0, floors untouched; the same tree re-measured by the independent session read 56.60/48.46/47.61/61.70 and the pre-recompute run read 56.72/48.66/47.71/61.83 — §12-22④ jitter, coverage is not an anchor) / `lint` **0 errors**, 29 legacy warnings, same four categories, zero new / `build` single file **2,021.74 kB · gzip 591.22 kB** (exactly 2,021,743 bytes; +2.97/+0.82 kB over v2.8.10 = merge-gates + per-branch evaluation + greyed authoring surface and window; version string still embedded exactly once).
- **The third gate ran twice, and the second run also corrected the first one's environment**: the independent recompute found three real gaps, all fixed inside this cut — (a) the group-gate column had **export-side tests missing** (only import was covered), (b) the header comment lookup `header.findIndex(h => h.endsWith('门槛'))` grabbed only the **first** matching column, so inserting 「技能门槛」 silently **moved the syntax note off the per-effect gate column** (now `forEach`, both gate columns get it), (c) the "activation is not wasted" assertion was half-tautological (`consumedSkills ?? []` passes when the field is absent) and now **re-dispatches the activation and requires the same `SKILL_CONDITION_UNMET`, never `SKILL_ALREADY_ACTIVATED`**. That session also reported a bogus "diff scope out of control" because it ran its git commands against a **different repository root** (a 3.x offline-package repo on this machine); re-verified here = this repo's `git rev-parse --show-toplevel` = `G:/THREE_KINGDOMS/Qoder/2.0`, 29 entries, `matchSetup`/fixtures/`domain` untouched. Durable rule registered (§12-63): **an independent recompute's git claims must come with its own `--show-toplevel` output** — a recompute session is as fallible as the implementation session, and this is now the fourth distinct flavour of "narration instead of the write point".
- **Both anchors verbatim, structurally**: the official pool's 95 generals contain **zero `effectMode:'choice'` and zero `conditions`**, the repo DIY fixture likewise, and every new field is optional ⇒ B10 `npm run ai-battle -- --games 300 --seed 1` run twice on the final tree = won=300 / exhausted=0 / VIOLATIONS=0, winners **{"1":112,"2":188}**, five-faction ledger identical to §12-43① cell by cell; B11 `… --diy-fixture --skill 0` = **{"1":108,"2":192}** with the three-faction ledger matching the v2.8.9 registration.
- **Real-browser E2E** (dev 5173, hot-seat, **no developer mode — this session does not hold the passphrase**, zero save/download dialogs): 12 DIY generals authored through `poolGenerals()` + `distributeDraftGenerals()` → visible on the draft screen → `confirmDraft` into the seat pool → deployed → the choice window opened: DOM readings show the failing branch **present but inert** (`disabled=true`, `title="不满足发动门槛：手牌≥9"`, label 「✕ 选项2：摸三张牌（不满足：手牌≥9）」), clicking it changes nothing (window still open, `pendingChoice` uncleared — i.e. not a fake success), clicking the live branch really settles (hand 0→1, `pendingChoice` cleared, `rngState` not consumed twice), zero console errors. Scene cleaned afterwards (authored list and both edit keys emptied, `resetGame()` back to menu). ## [2.8.10] - 2026-09-29

Wording-correction cut — **"a general with a blank identity never locks" was ambiguous and is now restated as "blank identity = *currently* no identity = no lock key *while it stays blank*"** (a user ruling, 2026-09-29; zero behaviour change).

- **The correction itself**: identity is an *editable* field — the user has ruled (and §H1 records) that it can go 有→无 and A→B at any later time. Blank therefore means "no lock key **while it stays blank**", not "permanently exempt": fill an identity in later and the card is back under the lock. The old "无身份 ⇒ 永不锁" shorthand came from *my* cut summaries, not from the contract table (grid 8 already said 不锁); it is corrected hereafter and a durable test criterion was registered — *whenever a rule says "does not apply in state X", say whether that is a judgement about the current state or a permanent property; "never" belongs only to genuinely frozen fields (`id`, `source`).*
- **The code was already right** — nothing had to change. `identity` is not in `FROZEN_GENERAL_FIELDS` (`generalProvenance.ts:21`, that set is exactly `['id','source']`); `lockKeyOf` is a pure function of the *current* identity (`identity.ts:45-49`); the editor really does offer 无身份 and re-selection from the registry. ## [2.8.9] - 2026-09-29

Foundation cut 4, **option A** — **DIY-shaped content now reaches automated verification through a repo-committed fixture and its own anchor, while the official-pool anchor stays byte-identical** (§H2's "developer-mode DIY automation = to be built" item turns from paper into code; touches `src/ai/`; a *non-content* cut ⇒ hard anchor = B10 verbatim, plus a **new** anchor B11 for the fixture pool).

- **Why a file instead of the player's own list**: the standing rule says anything that enters automated verification must be fully rebuildable from fixed files in the repository, and `localStorage` is a mutable player environment, never a CI input. So the DIY route to the engine is a **source file**, not the browser's authored-general list — the second half of that rule (option B: editor export → repo file) stays a deferred, filed-only task (#38) and is not started.
- **The fixture**: `src/ai/fixtures/diyGeneralFixture.ts` — 12 hard-coded DIY generals (Wei / Shu / Wu × 4, title 「仓库固定样本」, ids `D-fix-<key>`) each carrying **one editor-shaped skill** (`effects[].{trigger,runtime}`, one with a `conditions` gate, one with two effects). Every card is built through `createAuthoredGeneral` — §H1's *only* existence path — with an injected deterministic `randomId`, so the ids are reproducible rather than random UUIDs. A `console`-level guard throws if any spec cannot be authored.
- **Anti-vacuousness is a test, not a claim**: `diyGeneralFixture.test.ts` (7 cases) asserts per card that the compiler skipped **nothing** (`skipped` must equal `[]`) *and* that at least one definition survives, so a fixture card whose skill quietly fails to compile cannot pass. Plus: the gate reaches the compiled definition, the twin-effect skill yields ≥2 definitions, the runtime type set is exactly the nine supported types, all ids are `D-fix-*`/unique/acceptable-as-authored-records/absent from the ledger, `type` derives from hp, and the three factions each hold ≥4.
- **One seam, one writer**: `MatchConfig.poolSource?: 'official' | 'diy-fixture'` with two pure functions `generalsForPoolSource()` / `factionsForPoolSource()`; all three pool-reading sites inside `buildMatchState` now go through them. The default is the official pool **by identical reference** (`toBe` pinned), and `factionsForPoolSource(undefined)` returns `allFactions` in original order — so the official path consumes the seeded RNG exactly as before, which is what makes B10 verbatim a meaningful proof rather than luck. The **only** writer of `poolSource` is `battleCli.ts --diy-fixture`; the hash contract (`battleHash.ts`) does not carry it, so the browser practice window cannot select the fixture even in developer mode. Independent recompute: `poolGenerals` / `localStorage` / `authoredGenerals` appear inside `src/ai/**` only in comments, and `poolSource` has zero references outside `src/ai/`.
- **Two anchors side by side**: B10 unchanged (again reproduced twice this round: won=300, exhausted=0, VIOLATIONS=0, winners **{"1":112,"2":188}**, five-faction ledger identical to §12-43①). New **B11** = `npm run ai-battle -- --games 300 --seed 1 --diy-fixture --skill 0` → won=300 / 0 / 0, winners **{"1":108,"2":192}**, only three factions (Wei 217 seats 121 wins, Shu 193/87, Wu 190/92 — 217+193+190 = 600 = 2 players × 300 games, ledger self-consistent), three passes identical, and the report header prints a source line stating B10 does not apply.

The fixture cards already carry editor-shaped skills, so injection is switched off and every trigger counted by B11 belongs to the fixture itself (300 games: 蓄粮 42 / 双略 32 / 回报 7 / 转赠 6 / 固守 4 / 节用 4 / 抚伤 3 / 焚粮 3 / 缴械 2).

## [2.8.8] - 2026-09-29

N2 cut — **two locks in the general editor: a gold one the system owns and a white one the player owns, computed independently and never merged into one message** (§H8's N2 half turns from paper contract into code; touches `src/`; a *non-content* cut ⇒ hard anchor = B10 verbatim).

- **One write gate, two independent computations**: `src/domain/generalPolicy.ts` gains `mayWriteGeneral(id, {developerMode, lockedIds})` — the repository-wide *only* write gate. It first asks §H3's system layer (`mayModifyGeneral`), then the player's own white lock; the two are computed separately and combined only here, **stricter wins**. The refusal reason is now two named values — `OFFICIAL_READ_ONLY` vs `USER_LOCKED` — kept apart in every store result (`deniedLock` / `rejected`) and in every notice, because "the system forbids it" and "you locked it yourself" are different sentences to the user.
- **The switch can never trap itself**: toggling the white lock goes through `mayToggleGeneralLock`, which reads the **system layer only**. A white lock never blocks its own removal, so the protection cannot become a one-way door.
- **A lock blocks future writes, it never deactivates content**: editing, deleting, overwriting (Excel "attach to an existing general"), disabling, the identity-rename cascade and text import all pass the same gate and write **nothing** when refused — not to state, not to `localStorage`. The assembly view never reads `lockedIds`, so a locked card that already carries an edit still shows `✏️` (not `🚫`) and still plays that edit. "Lock = suspend" would be inventing gameplay, which §H8 forbids.
- **Residence = this machine**: `three_kingdoms_locked_generals` (one local id list) — not in source files, `EngineState`, replays or CI inputs ⇒ a non-content cut, no anchor change; `resetGame` keeps it (player asset ≠ match state), and deleting an authored card clears its dead id's lock so no orphan lock squats a slot.
- **A contract clause that was structurally unreachable**: §H8 requires gold and white locks to be *distinguishable in the interface*, but the editor entry was rendered only under `developerMode` — so on any reachable screen the gold-lock predicate was constantly false and that line of code could never run. The entry is now always rendered; enforcement stays in the store guard (v2.8.6's three layers). Live check: a non-developer sees all 95 officials gold-locked with **zero** white-lock switches, and `enableDeveloperMode('0'.repeat(64))` from the console is refused by the digest gate.
- **Bug caught while changing a return type**: `removeAuthoredGeneral` went from `boolean` to `{ok, denial}`, and the old `setX(f() ? success : failure)` treats *any object* as truthy — a lock-refused delete would have reported "已删除自建将领". Handlers now branch on `result.ok` explicitly. Durable rule: when a write action's return type is upgraded, grep every call site; TypeScript will not warn.
- **Input surface**: per-row white-lock switch (never offered on a gold-locked card), two-colour markers that cannot impersonate each other, batch 锁定/解锁 in the multi-select toolbar with refused officials named in a persistent strip (the right-hand panel has no footing in multi-select), a 「🔓 在此解锁」 escape inside the white-lock notice, and a plain-language banner explaining both locks for anyone entering without developer mode.
- **Tests 671 → 696 (69 → 71 files, +25)**: new `store/gameStoreEditorActions.locks.test.ts` (14 — every write class refused, unlock restores writeability, double toggle never self-locks, non-developers cannot white-lock a gold card, a dev's own white lock does stop them, delete counts as a write and clears the dead id's lock, batch results keep the two denial classes apart, text import names each class, rename cascade leaves a locked card byte-identical, and three assembly-doesn't-read-locks cases incl. persistence round-trip and corrupt-save ⇒ empty set), new `components/SkillEditor.locks.test.tsx` (7 — lock-on-row-keeps-storage-and-disables-save, unlock escape writes again, gold card has no switch, colours don't impersonate, batch refusal naming, "lock doesn't suspend" still shows ✏️, and the non-developer reaches the editor from the codex), plus 4 policy-root cases in `generalPolicy.test.ts`.
- **Five gates on the final tree**: `check` 0 errors / `npm test` **696 · 71 files** / coverage 56.19/48/47.16/61.32 over floors 42/34/34/47 (floors unchanged) / `lint` **0 errors**, 29 legacy warnings (zero new) / `build` single file **2,015.28 kB · gzip 589.52 kB**.
- **Non-content hard anchor held verbatim, twice**: ai-battle 300 games seed 1 → won=300, exhausted=0, VIOLATIONS=0, winners **{"1":112,"2":188}**, all five faction ledger lines identical to §12-43①.
- **Real-browser E2E (dev server :5173, no save/download dialogs touched)**: non-developer path end-to-end — codex entry → banner → 95 gold locks / no white switches → creating a general is forced to `D-*`/DIY → row lock lands in state *and* localStorage → notice + 「在此解锁」 + save button disabled → unlock restores writeability → batch lock (1 authored locked, 95 officials refused and named) → batch unlock clears → **hand-planting `wei_001` into the lock file and reloading still renders gold only, with no switch** (the two computations cannot impersonate each other; a tampered file opens no second transition path). Residue cleared, server killed and port re-checked. **Honest boundary**: the developer-side view (everything unlocked by default, officials white-lockable too) is covered by unit tests only — this session does not hold the developer passphrase.

## [2.8.7] - 2026-09-29

Content-era foundation cut 3 (N1) — **Excel import becomes a two-entry flow: modify *or* create, and an unresolvable row is never silently guessed** (§H8 turns from paper contract into code; touches `src/`; a *non-content* cut ⇒ hard anchor = B10 verbatim).

- **The dead-branch bug this cut kills**: the old `resolveGeneralByNameFaction` resolved an Excel row's `name + faction` against the pool, and when the faction didn't match it fell through to `return sameName[0]` — silently applying an edit to a *different-faction same-name card*. The official pool has four such pairs (司马懿 / 邓艾 / 钟会 / 张春华, Wei↔Jin), so this was live, not hypothetical (confirmed by direct test before the cut). Replaced by `resolveGeneralForImport(name, factionText, pool)` returning a strict three-state result — `unique` (auto-hit) / `ambiguous` (multiple same-name ⇒ present candidates, never auto-pick) / `missing` (nothing in the pool ⇒ can be created).
- **One pass, two phases (no per-row popups)**: `parseRowPerSkillSheet` now takes an optional `pool` and returns `{entries, parseWarnings, unresolved}`. Uniquely-resolved rows apply immediately through the per-row store mutations (the single-mutation path established in v2.8.6); unresolvable blocks are collected into `UnresolvedImportBlock` — carrying the raw name/faction/hp/atk, the skill set, the source **row-number range**, the resolution reason and the candidate list — and deferred to a pending panel. The `continue` that used to skip a general row was removed, so a single row can now carry both general attributes *and* its first skill.
- **Component (two-phase UI)**: `SkillEditor` gains a `pendingImports` state and a blue pending list — each row shows name/faction/hp/row-range/reason/skill-count, with a top **✅ Create-all** button plus a per-row three-way choice (create as a DIY general / attach to an existing general to modify / skip). Backward compatible: `resolveGeneralByNameFaction` kept as a wrapper for the simple-format import, and `parseLegacyDetailedRow` adapted to the three-state result (returns raw fields when unresolved).
- **Backfill round — a second fork caught by self-recompute**: the three-state judgement was correct at the parser *entry* but `flushCurrent` re-derived the block's `resolution`/`candidates` from scratch, hard-coding them to `'missing'` / `[]` at the *exit* — so the pending panel would show "multiple cards share this name" as "no such person in the library", and the "attach to existing" dropdown came up empty (a §H8 criterion-② violation, same species as §12-55's display-vs-effect fork). Fixed by having `currentUnresolved` carry the three-state result through; +7 regression tests pin all branches (faction mismatch ⇒ ambiguous with all same-name candidates, same-faction same-name with distinct identities ⇒ candidates scoped to that faction, blank faction + multiple ⇒ ambiguous, unknown name ⇒ missing, unique ⇒ lands in entries, plus direct three-state root tests) using **self-built fixtures** — §H8's read-code fact is that the official pool's same-name pairs are cross-faction, so "name + faction" is unique there and the candidate path cannot be exercised against the official pool. `package.json` bumped 2.8.6 → 2.8.7 (the feat round forgot it).
- **Tests → 671 (69 files)**: `skillExcelParsers.test.ts` 8 → 15. **Five gates on the final tree**: `check` 0 errors / `npm test` 671 · 69 files / coverage ≥ floor 42/34/34/47 (measured 55.32/46.94/45.24/60.53) / `lint` 0 errors, 29 legacy warnings (zero new) / `build` single file 2,008.48 kB · gzip 587.99 kB. **Non-content hard anchor held verbatim**: ai-battle 300 games seed 1 → won=300, exhausted=0, VIOLATIONS=0, winners **{"1":112,"2":188}**.
- **Remote CI (verified after the user made the repo public to clear a private-Actions billing block)**: CI **#153** (run `36442939229`, registration commit `d475425`) Success, 2m17s; CI **#154** (run `36445429374`, the billing-note commit) Success, 2m14s; the earlier mis-reported CI **#152** (run `36404736608`, `63eb1d3`) confirmed Success (2m38s) by opening its detail page. Root cause of the mis-report recorded as a durable CI-check discipline (§12-56③). **Honest boundary (PENDING)**: the pending panel's three-way interaction has **no real-browser E2E** — the editor is gated behind the developer-mode passphrase this session does not hold; the parser layer is pinned by the 7 new tests, the UI layer awaits user on-device regression. Tag `v2.8.7` stays on the registration commit `d475425` (the backfill hangs on the same version line, per the v2.8.6 precedent).

## [2.8.6] - 2026-09-28

Content-era foundation cut 2 — **nobody edits an official general from outside developer mode** (§H3 turns from paper contract into code; touches `src/`; a *non-content* cut ⇒ hard anchor = B10 verbatim).

- **One judgement root, no second opinion**: new `src/domain/generalPolicy.ts` — `mayModifyGeneral(id, {developerMode})` reads the *id namespace only*: a legacy repository-ledger id (`wei_001` shape) is official and writable **only** in developer mode; `G-*` / `D-*` local ids are writable in any session (local content reaches the ledger through human review, not through a permission flag); anything whose shape can't be recognised falls to the **stricter** side. `denialMessage()` is the single source of the refusal sentence, `partitionEditMap` / `partitionIdSet` split any overlay map into permitted/blocked **without touching the source object**.
- **The layer that was actually missing (② store guard)**: before this cut the *only* gate was UI conditional rendering, so a direct store call could rewrite an official card. `updateSkillEdit` / `updateGeneralEdit` / `toggleDisabledGeneral` now return an `EditDecision` and, when refused, write **nothing** — not to state, not to localStorage (pinned by asserting `loadPersisted*()` after each refusal). Batch delete, batch disable and text import run through the same guard and report `applied` / `rejected`; `renameIdentity`'s cascade now skips official generals so their historical overlays stay byte-identical.
- **The bypass this cut closed**: the Excel import path used to `useGameStore.setState({skillEdits, generalEdits})` plus two hand-written `localStorage.setItem` calls — i.e. it drove around every guard. It is re-plumbed to call the single-row mutations per row, collecting refused names into the same notice. Durable rule for future writes: import / batch / migration paths must go through per-row mutation actions, never `setState`.
- **Assembly-time fallback (③), with one honest deviation from the contract**: `getGeneralWithEdits` refuses to read an official general's two overlay tables outside developer mode, `effectiveDisabledGenerals()` filters the disable list, `blockedEdits()` names what got stopped. The contract text said "throw the whole batch"; the implementation **stops applying and reports instead of throwing** — throwing would crash the game for any player carrying a historical official edit, turning a governance action into gameplay breakage, while the three required states (data preserved / application forbidden / conflict flagged) hold without it. Recorded for the user's veto in HANDOFF §12-54④.
- **Duplicate merge logic collected in**: `Codex.tsx`'s private 13-line overlay merge is deleted; `GameBoard.tsx` / `TestArena.tsx` no longer read `skillEdits[g.id] ?? g.skills` for display (a *display* bypass that would show a stopped edit as live). One merge path, policy-filtered.
- **Input surface (①) tells the truth now**: the save button is disabled for an official general, the refusal sentence appears inline and persists, rows carry `🔒` (read-only) and `🚫` (edit still in the archive, no longer applied) markers, the overview shows `effectiveDisabledGenerals().size` and a blocked-edits card, and the codex page carries a banner counting stopped overlays with "原数据仍保留".
- **Four judgment calls made on the user's behalf, each vetoable**: (i) "official" = the repository ledger only, so a `G-*` official *draft* stays editable outside developer mode; (ii) disabling or deleting an official overlay counts as a modification and is refused; (iii) `developerMode` is not persisted, so a developer's own official edits stop applying after a refresh and return verbatim on re-entry; (iv) the editor remains developer-only — opening it to normal players belongs to the N2 protection-lock cut.
- **Tests 644 → 664 (66 → 69 files, +20)**: new `generalPolicy.test.ts` (6 — ledger readonly for a player / writable for a dev, `G-`/`D-` always editable, unknown shapes incl. `''` go stricter, the denial sentence, partition leaves the source untouched), `gameStoreEditorActions.policy.test.ts` (10 — refusal writes nothing at any layer, dev-mode and DIY success paths, batch `applied`/`rejected`, text import `{count:0, rejected:[关羽]}`, rename cascade skipping officials, blocked-then-restored assembly, `poolGenerals()`, and "the ledger itself is never rewritten by the diff layer" — that last one **snapshotted by value** so it cannot be tautological), `SkillEditor.readOnly.test.tsx` (4 — UI cells for read-only / stopped-but-preserved / editable-authored, plus the two-sided strike-through comparison). Four pre-existing files gained `developerMode: true` because they deliberately write official overlays — new behaviour, not a regression.
- **Five gates on the final tree (re-run after the recompute fixes)**: `check` 0 errors / **664 tests, 69 files** / coverage ≥ floor with `generalPolicy.ts` at **100/100/100/100** / `lint` **0 errors, 29 legacy warnings (zero new)** / `build` single file 2,000.73 kB.

Test residue cleared from the three localStorage keys and re-verified after reload.
- **What this cut deliberately did not do**: no new gameplay, no new primitive, no content; the Excel import *dual entry* (modify + create, §H8 / N1) is the next cut, and so is the N2 lock that needs a player-visible editor.
- **Independent recompute (third gate) run by a fresh context-free session, and what it caught**: verdict = deliverable, all three red lines clear (single transition path — the only persistence funnel `editorPersistence.ts` sits behind the guarded actions; `git diff --stat` empty over `src/ai`, `src/core`, `src/data`; no batch content promotion). It found two defects measured against this cut's own standard, both fixed in a follow-up commit: **(a) a tautological assertion** — `policy.test.ts` compared `fromLedger.hp` against `ledger.hp`, i.e. two references to the *same object*, so in-place mutation of the ledger would have moved both sides and stayed green; now the pre-edit values are snapshotted **by value** before the patch, plus a `not.toBe(GENERAL_PATCH.hp)` guard so the comparison can matter. **(b) an inverted display bypass** — the editor row struck an official general through using the **raw** `disabledGenerals` set while the game reads `effectiveDisabledGenerals()`, so a developer-era disable kept showing as inactive after a refresh that had already made it playable; rows and the overview count now read the filtered view, pinned by a two-sided test (same raw record: player view = no strike + count 0, developer view = strike + count 1). Durable rule: **anything that displays a state must read the view assembly actually applies, never the raw archive set.** Post-fix readings: **664 tests / 69 files**, other four gates unchanged, anchor still verbatim `{"1":112,"2":188}`. Registered as `PROJECT_HANDOFF.md` §12-55; the fix and its registration pushed as **CI #151** (run `36404208110`, **Success**, 3m 58s, four jobs green) — tag `v2.8.6` stays on the registration commit `b756369` per house convention.
- **Remote CI verified**: GitHub Actions **CI #147** (run `36400599591`) **Success** on the registration commit `b756369` (parent = feat `c7a44d7`), total **4m 24s** — all four jobs green (`test (22)`, `test (24)`, `lint` 1m 26s, `build` 56s). Pushed **directly** (no proxy needed this time); annotated tag `v2.8.6` pushed alongside.

## [2.8.5] - 2026-09-28

Content-era foundation cut 1 — **where a new general comes from** (§H1/§H2 turn from paper contract into code; touches `src/`; a *non-content* cut ⇒ hard anchor = B10 verbatim; gates ① and ③ of the gameplay-knife protocol both fired and are backfilled). Before this version the game could not create a general at all — `createGeneral` was module-private and served the 95 repository cards; after it, a player can, and the number that card gets **can never be changed again**.

- **The cut started with the user correcting me**: 「身份标识应该是允许修改的才对，不然我创建的时候不小心把身份选错了或者忘记加身份了怎么办」 ⇒ **the frozen set is `{id, source}`, two fields, not three**. `identity` sits with name/faction/hp/attacks/armor/skills/title as an editable content field — which is also what the editor already did today, so freezing it would have been a regression dressed up as a guarantee.
- **One door for minting**: new `src/domain/generalProvenance.ts` — `createAuthoredGeneral(input, source, randomId?)` is the only path by which a general record can come into existence (the recompute session grepped for a second one and found none: the store writes `authoredGenerals` in exactly one action). Three refusals, all input-surface: blank name / hp not a finite number > 0 / no random-id source on this machine. The derived stats (`hp>=4 ⇒ 武将`, the two attack defaults) reuse `data/generals.ts`'s existing card shape, so **no gameplay rule was invented**; the engine simply accepted a zero-skill authored card when one reached a real match.
- **Disjoint namespaces**: `G-*` = official **local draft** (developer mode only), `D-*` = player-made (the default; a non-developer asking for `official` silently gets `DIY`). All 95 repository cards are legacy-shaped ids (`wei_001`), pinned one by one in a test, which is what makes "a local file shadows an official card" structurally impossible — and what makes **`G-`/`D-` reserved for local content forever** (promoted into §H1 and AGENTS.md: a future repository card minted as `G-x` would pass the acceptance test **without any test going red**).
- **`identity` is written explicitly at creation** (unset ⇒ the name, `__none__` ⇒ `''` = never locks). That closes the trap the correction exposes: `identityOf()` derives identity from the name when none is stored, so renaming a no-identity general quietly moves its lock key. Officials keep the derived form with zero data change, which keeps B10's input surface untouched.
- **Restore is fail-closed, in three places sharing one predicate**: `loadPersistedGeneralEdits` strips `id`/`source` out of any persisted patch and `console.warn`s what it dropped; `loadAuthoredGenerals` admits a record only if its id namespace and `source` agree, its name is non-blank, and it is not a ledger id — a bad record is dropped whole; `resetGame` **does not** clear the authored list, because local content is a player asset, not match state. A write the user cannot see was dropped is the worst failure shape, hence the warn rather than a silent filter.
- **The load-bearing boundary — two pools, not one**: `poolGenerals()` (repository **+** authored) feeds **only** the browser's two draft sites; `ai/matchSetup.ts` and every `src/ai/**` consumer keep reading `allGenerals`, and `battleCli → battleRunner → matchSetup` never imports the store, so the local list has **no entry point on the Node side**. Merging the two reads would push `localStorage` into B10 and break the user's standing rule that anything feeding automated verification must be rebuildable from fixed repository files. Excel export stays ledger-only for the same reason: an export→import round trip can neither re-mint ids nor launder local content into the official surface.
- **Delete leaves no orphans**: the 🗑 action runs three gates (is it an authored namespace → is it actually in the local list → only then clear `generalEdits` / `skillEdits` / `disabledGenerals` keyed by that id) and refuses a repository id at the first gate. Cleanup addresses by `id`, never by name or identity, because two cards sharing a name is a legal shape here.
- **Input surface**: 「➕ 新建将领」 opens a panel (name / faction / hp / an identity dropdown offering *same as name*, *no identity*, and the registry / a source dropdown that only appears in developer mode / a plain-language paragraph explaining draft-vs-repository), list rows carry a provenance badge (自建 / 官方草稿) and the delete affordance shows on local cards only.
- **Tests 619 → 644 (66 files, +25)**: new `generalProvenance.test.ts` (15 — frozen-set shape *including* "identity is not in it", strip-and-report, the stricter `sourceOf` default, all 95 ledger ids legacy, namespace follows source, the three identity spellings at creation, two same-name cards, the 武将/文将 defaults, each refusal independently, real-RNG uniqueness, five negative cases for the acceptance test) and `gameStore.authoredGenerals.test.ts` (10 — create→state→localStorage→reload agree in three places, local pool has the new card and loses none of the 95, delete refuses a repository id, non-developer `official` falls back to `DIY/D-`, developer gets `G-`, `resetGame` keeps the list, corrupt blobs filtered, a rename leaves the identity lock key alone, a persisted patch carrying `id`/`source` is stripped on load with exactly one warn, deleting a card clears all three overlays). **Coverage floors untouched.**
- **Five gates on the final tree**: `check` 0 errors / **644 tests, 66 files** / coverage **54.63 / 46.77 / 44.91 / 60.05** (Stmts/Branch/Funcs/Lines) ≥ floors 42/34/34/47, exit 0 / lint 0 errors, 30 legacy warnings, zero new / build **1,996.81 kB, gzip 585.22 kB** (+7.61 / +1.97 kB over v2.8.4 — the provenance contract, the acceptance filters and the create panel). Version `2.8.4 → 2.8.5` (the version number lives in `package.json` only).
- **Non-content hard anchor held three times**: ai-battle 300 games seed 1 → won=300, exhausted=0, VIOLATIONS=0, winners **{"1":112,"2":188}** verbatim against B10, per-faction ledger matching §12-43① cell for cell, run again before and after the browser session with identical readings.
- **Real-browser E2E** (dev server on :5173, single instance, no save/download dialog touched): the two refusal sentences appear verbatim; a real click creates 「碧谷·蜀」 as `D-b2048e57-…` with badge 自建 and exactly **one** 🗑 in a 96-row list; the developer-mode source dropdown offers both `DIY/玩家自制` and `official/官方（本地草稿）` and the latter mints `G-f61d89cf-…` with badge 官方草稿; **reloading the page** restores the same card under **the same id** with `developerMode` back to false (the dev flag is not persisted by design); a persisted `{faction:'晋'}` overlay resolves **by id**; and that authored card genuinely appears on the live draft screen (`✓📜碧谷❤️3⚔️1🏹2`) and reaches `players[0].generalPool` through `confirmDraft` with an `instanceId`. Cleanup: authored list back to `[]`, both edit keys and the disabled list emptied, pool 95, dev server killed and `:5173` re-checked idle.
- **Gate ③ (independent recompute) backfilled**: a fresh read-only session's verdict = **commit-ready, no red-line violation**, with per-item evidence for the single mint path, `identity` staying editable, the AI/CLI chain never seeing local content, deletion's three gates, `resetGame`'s asset/match split, and "25 assertions that actually assert" (it re-ran vitest itself). It derived B10's stability from the three anchor inputs (pool contents, RNG consumption order, whether the change reaches the CLI) and left two follow-ups, both registered not fixed: the `G-`/`D-` reserved-prefix corollary (now a standing rule) and **Codex / the Excel import path not showing authored cards** (a display gap, not a contract violation) → foundation cuts 2 and 3. **GPT external review: not requested** — no two-way judgement was open: the frozen set was fixed by the user's correction on the spot, and the namespaces and the two-layer notion of "official" were already written down in §H1/§H2.
- **What this cut deliberately did not do**: §H3's three-layer enforcement (the store mutation guard is still the missing layer — the only gate today remains `Codex.tsx`'s conditional rendering) = foundation cut 2; §H8's import-as-create-plus-modify = cut 3; the editor's protection lock = N2; and promoting a draft into the repository, which would add real content and therefore **re-anchor B10 → B11**. None of that happened here.
- **Remote CI verified**: GitHub Actions **CI #141** (run `36387017064`) **Success** on the docs commit `edfd1ba`, total **3m 58s** — both matrix jobs (`test (22)`, `test (24)`) report **66 files / 644 tests passing** (read off the run summary's Vitest Test Report, i.e. the same figures as the local final tree), `lint` 1m8s, `build` 54s, annotations still the 14 pre-existing legacy warnings (Node 20 deprecation ×2, React hooks ×12). **This round pushed directly on the first attempt** (master `a2cdfad..edfd1ba` and tag `v2.8.5`, no proxy needed), which also carried the three documentation-only commits `f449de3`/`33872f4`/`9bed659` deferred since the prerequisite rulings round — that "push with the next cut" tab is now closed. Chain: feat `fcd8aa8` (10 files, +637/−18) → docs `edfd1ba` → annotated tag `v2.8.5` on the registration commit → backfill `558c80e`, whose own run is verified in-session too: **CI #142** (run `36387677901`) **Success** on `558c80e`, 3m 11s, both matrix jobs again 66 files / 644 tests, `lint` 1m6s, `build` 47s, same 14 annotations — and that push also went out **directly on the first attempt**. Registration closes at this layer.

### Corrections registered the same day (2.8.5 follow-up, **docs only — `src/`, tests and the baseline anchor untouched**)

- **"95 cards" was written as if it were an invariant; it is a snapshot.** User: 「现在的95张卡作为官方卡池，这个数量并不是不会变动的，我以后也会更新增加官方卡池」 ⇒ every "official 95 generals" reading in the docs is *as of v2.8.5*; **growing the official pool is a content cut and therefore re-anchors** (B10 → next anchor, old readings kept as history). The genuinely count-independent invariant is *shape*: repository ids must never take the `G-`/`D-` prefixes. Verified by reading the test: it iterates the ledger asserting `idNamespace === 'legacy'` per card and **does not hard-code a length**, so expansion cannot turn it falsely red.
- **"Only the official pool can reach the AI / automated verification" was my mis-transcription of the user's rule, and the causal direction is now fixed.** User: 「目前的游戏只有标准模式，标准模式不允许使用DIY卡，所以不会出现AI使用DIY卡自动对战的情况，但是我作为开发者，在开发者模式下还是需要让AI使用新建的将领卡和技能进行自动化验证的」 ⇒ ① there is no "AI auto-battles a DIY card" scenario today **because of a gameplay rule** (standard mode forbids DIY), not because of a permissions or verification ban; ② "DIY generals in AI auto-battles" is **not built** (it waits on free mode) — the §H2 line that filed it under *"explicitly not doing"* has been rewritten; ③ **developer-mode AI verification over newly authored generals and skills is a registered requirement**, and the only admissible shapes are a **repo-fixed DIY fixture plus its own anchor**, or a human-merged entry into `src/data/generals.ts` (a normal content cut, re-anchoring). Wiring `poolGenerals()` — which includes `localStorage` — into `ai/matchSetup` is banned by the standing rule, since that would make a player's browser a CI input.
- **New durable lesson (third appearance of the same family of distortion, in a new direction)**: earlier rounds mistook an external review's *candidate model* for *current fact*; this round a **structurally-not-yet-reachable path** was read as **the user's intent**. Rule adopted: any statement of the form "the user does not want / this will never be done" may only be backed by the user's own words; if a route is simply not built yet, it must be written as **pending**.
- **Surfaces rewritten** (no stale wording left standing as a basis): `PROJECT_ARCH_MAP.md` §H2 (two new lines + the landing note now says the pool split is *current shape, not the end state*), `PROJECT_HANDOFF.md` §3 items ⑥/⑬ + §9 + new §12-53 + §12-52 ⑶, `AGENTS.md` (standing-rule tail and the "two general pools" bullet's reason), both history documents' v2.8.5 chapters, and the project memory. `src/` untouched ⇒ **the five gates and the anchor were deliberately not re-run** — there was no code to verify; CI #141/#142 readings remain authoritative. **This docs commit was pushed the same day rather than riding the next cut** (`ae797ef`; direct push timed out so it used the one-shot `127.0.0.1:10808` proxy, still no persistent git config) — the wrong rule was live on the remote and any session reading the docs in the meantime would have worked from it. Remote CI verified green: **CI #144** (run `36389715800`, **4m 9s**, all six jobs completed successfully); the backfill commit `193ab15` verified green in its own run = **CI #145** (run `36390502588`, **3m 36s**).

## [2.8.4] - 2026-09-28

2.8 cut 5 — **the input surface speaks plain Chinese now** (刀 C, touches `src/`; a *non-content* cut ⇒ hard anchor = B10 verbatim; pure presentation wording ⇒ gate ① of the gameplay-knife protocol does not fire, gate ③ is delivered by three replacement witnesses). The four coinages the user named are gone from every surface he reads and types — and the real work of this cut turned out to be **backwards compatibility**, not renaming.

- **What changed**: `EQUIP_STRIP` 剥离装备 → **拆掉装备**, `REVEAL` 观顶 → **看牌堆顶**, `DECK_PLACE` 置牌入堆 → **放回牌堆**, `TARGET` 被作用者 → **目标**. Surfaces covered: the Excel 类型/目标 cell values **and both dropdown validation strings**, the editor's two dropdowns, the preview sentences (「拆掉 N 张装备卡」/「把 N 张手牌放回牌堆」), the gate subject word + `GATE_SYNTAX_HINT` + the live gate echo, and **one card description** (`src/data/generals.ts:296`, 典韦·强袭).
- **Deliberately not a batch rename**: the same tables still hold 发放 / 获得护甲 / 场上将领 / 本次伤害 / 纯描述. Those were not among the four words the user named, and choosing words is product judgement, not engineering judgement — so they stay, registered rather than "tidied".
- **Compatibility is the design**: new `LEGACY_TYPE_LABELS` / `LEGACY_TARGET_LABELS` (Excel side) and the `被作用者` / `受击者` entries kept in `SUBJECT_ALIASES` (gate side). Parse order is fixed at **enum name → current label → legacy word**, while reverse-writing (structure → text/cell) **only ever emits the current label** — a one-way door. Why it is mandatory: the user's own `.xlsx` files are the long-lived asset, exported before this version with the old words in them; a rename with no aliases would make his old files **fail on import overnight**, and in the worst possible shape — silently read as "no type / no target", so the skill still imports, still shows, and simply never settles again.
- **Word tables live in exactly two files** (so a word can never differ between the UI and the export): `skillExcelFormat.ts` (`runtimeEffectTypeLabels`, `runtimeTargetLabels`) and `skillGateText.ts` (`GATE_SUBJECT_LABELS`). Everything else derives from them — including `RUNTIME_TYPE_LIST` / `SETTLEABLE_RUNTIME_TYPE_LIST` / `RUNTIME_TARGET_LIST`. **Card text is the vocabulary's second home and is not derived from any table**: grepping only the label maps would have missed 典韦 (the one official card whose prose used this vocabulary).
- **Two new pins (619 tests / 64 files)**: `skillExcelFormat.test.ts` 「旧行话只进不出」 asserts every written table and every dropdown string contains the new words **and none of the four old ones**, plus **every dropdown option must be parseable by our own parser** (the v2.8.3 `readTriggerCell` discipline reused); `skillGateText.test.ts` asserts 「被作用者体力=1」 and 「受击者手牌>自身手牌」 parse **identically** to the new spellings and that the round-trip always writes 「目标…」. The existing hint-drift pin was strengthened too: `GATE_SYNTAX_HINT` must contain every value of every label table, close 写法→结构→写法 verbatim, and keep the "SELF writes no prefix" rule.
- **Five gates, re-run on the final tree**: `check` 0 errors / **619 tests, 64 files** / coverage snapshot **53.79 / 46.06 / 44.29 / 59.21** ≥ floors 42/34/34/47, exit 0, **floors untouched** (the delta versus v2.8.3's 53.79/46.08/44.29/59.2 is the known §12-22④ jitter, not an anchor; a second run on the final tree read 54.00 / 46.40 / 44.45 / 59.44, also exit 0) / lint 0 errors, 30 legacy warnings, zero new / build **1,989.20 kB, gzip 583.25 kB** — **+0.10 / +0.05 kB** over v2.8.3, i.e. two alias tables and two test pins, a delta that matches a wording-only cut. Version `2.8.3 → 2.8.4` (the version number lives in `package.json` only).
- **Non-content hard anchor held**: ai-battle 300 games seed 1 → won=300, exhausted=0, VIOLATIONS=0, winners **{"1":112,"2":188}** verbatim against B10, per-faction ledger matching §12-43① cell for cell, and two same-seed runs on the final tree identical except the timing line — "zero gameplay change" proven numerically.
- **Real-browser E2E** (dev server on :5173, single instance, **no save/download dialog touched anywhere**): a **6-column (v2-shape) workbook written in the OLD words** was generated on the fly (three Wei generals, effect types 「剥离装备／观顶／置牌入堆」, targets 「被作用者／自身／目标」) and uploaded through the page's real `<input type=file>` (`DataTransfer` + `change`) ⇒ the persistent warning list showed **zero entries** — not one "didn't understand" — while the store received `EQUIP_STRIP+TARGET` / `REVEAL+SELF` / `DECK_PLACE+TARGET`; opening that general in the editor shows the new option strings with the freshly imported row selected as 「拆掉装备」/「目标」 (the export side is the same table, so reverse-write can only produce new words); and typing the old words into the gate box 「被作用者体力=1，目标手牌>自身手牌」 echoes live 「看懂了：**目标**体力=1 且 **目标**手牌>手牌」. Developer mode was opened through the DEV-only `window.__TK__` hook — the passphrase stays unknown to me by design and in no file.

- **Cross-reference guard for future sessions**: the v2.6.x twelve-slot table titles and the §G content notes still say 观顶 / 置牌入堆 / 剥离装备 / 被作用者 — those are **shorthand for the same capability**, and from this version the input surface, Excel and card text all use the new words; the old ones survive only inside `LEGACY_*` / `SUBJECT_ALIASES` (accepted, never written). Read them as the new words and do not conclude the UI still shows them (written into ARCH_MAP §F).
